...to the planets, or similar to what is received from the actions of the planetary spheres. Its innate nature—that is, the quality upon which it was constructed—must be receptive to the influence of the planets. This applies to stones as well, because the nature of the stone from which a **talisman** <term>talisman: an object inscribed with astronomical symbols or signs intended to draw down and store cosmic power</term> is made needs to be strengthened. Once it reaches the limit of its dominant nature, its influence spreads and becomes manifest. For those who practice this branch of science, there is no way to achieve this other than by increasing the stone's inherent quality with external qualities similar to it, until it becomes dominant through its own power. This is similar to what is done with **electuaries** <term>electuary: a medicinal paste or conserve, often mixed with honey or syrup</term> such as **thériac** <term>thériac: a famous ancient "universal antidote" composed of many ingredients, believed to gain power through the synergy of its parts</term>, for example, which overcomes illness through the combined strength of its many medicinal ingredients. Within all these medicines, there are numerous corrective measures used in various crafts, not just in form—such as medicine, manufacturing, and other natural matters like talismans used to repel the plague and the interactions between different stones.

->(And remember the word regarding the second result)<-

In all your works, adhere to what is harmonious and avoid the discordant, which repels one another. The pivot of everything we have mentioned regarding this statement depends on your understanding of it.

### (Chapter)

Know that the ancients' arrangement of the degrees of the natures was revealed to them through this path, enabling them to know the ranks of the powers of medicines, foods, and other things. Subsequently, they compounded them with one another until the benefits and various effects of the medicines became apparent to them. It is necessary here to mention what is required of this knowledge, so I say:

The ancients differed concerning the nature of the "simple elements" and disputed their number. A group of them—the researchers—said that the simple natures and the primary mothers are **Heat, Cold, Moisture, and Dryness**. In reality, these are qualities and simple origins, and there was no disagreement on this. Then they named the compounds, which are our terms: "Hot," "Cold," "Moist," and "Dry." Our saying "Hot" refers to matter accompanied by heat, and the same applies to the rest. This is not the same as saying "Heat" or "Cold" itself. After this comes a second composition, which is our saying: **Hot-Dry, Hot-Moist, Cold-Dry, and Cold-Moist**. These are secondary in composition because it is clear they are not single units, as Heat is distinct from Dryness and Moisture; the same applies to Coldness and its pairings. Then follows a third composition: **Fire, Air, Water, and Earth**. This is a third compound in relation to the first simple quality and the first and second compounds. Then follows the fourth composition, which is found in individual bodies. In the world, this is divided into the four seasons: **Spring, Autumn, Winter, and Summer**. In humans and animals, these are the **four humors** <term>humors: the four bodily fluids—yellow bile, blood, black bile, and phlegm—thought to determine a person's health and temperament</term>: **Yellow Bile, Blood, Black Bile, and Phlegm**.

Know that the elements within a human being are much subtler than those in an animal, because the natures of animals are very dense. As for the natures in plants, they are what are called oils, dyes, sprouts, and roots. Similarly, this is the case for the natures of stones. Whoever considers what we have said regarding the natures of animals and humans should also apply this same analogy to the natures of plants and stones. One should consider plants to have a subtlety of nature similar to humans, while stones are like those of animals. This concludes the section. Beyond these compounds are the "constructed things," which are called the **compounds of compounds**. These have seven degrees and are the final compounds, such as medicines, buildings, and all other things in this state. If the types of natures—both simple and compound—are categorized within them, they are encompassed by seven divisions.

- ٤٧ -

Twenty-eight letters <note>Referring to the letters of the Arabic alphabet, often used in occult sciences to represent numerical values or cosmic principles</note>. Let us mention an example for them regarding the depiction of the image and the form, so that it may be a comprehensive collection of what was dispersed in the explanation according to this example, which is this:

<columns>2</columns>

We say that Heat, Cold, Dryness, and Moisture precede Fire, Air, Water, and Earth in many ways that are apparent through both logic and the senses. One reason is that Fire is described by Heat—as being hot and dry—but "Heat" itself is not described as being "Fire," nor is it a composite. Another reason is that every composite is either composed of the primary elements by which it is described, or it is a composite that has always been influenced by Heat before Fire, by Moisture before Air, by Cold before Water, and by Dryness before that—but this is understood through the method of analysis. The same applies to the liver, the spleen, the gallbladder, the heart, the lungs, the head, the legs, the hands, and all the remaining parts of the body.

Heat, as previously mentioned, arises from the continuous movement of the **sphere** <term>sphere: the celestial heavens which were believed to rotate eternally around the Earth</term>, while Cold comes from the direction of the center—that is, the Earth, which is called the "Center of the All." For this reason, it was the **Hyle** <term>Hyle: from the Greek word for 'matter,' referring here to the primal, formless substance that carries the potential for all creation</term> that contains the benefit of the entire universe. Cold contradicts Heat in all its states—namely in its nature, movement, and stillness. Just as the definition of Heat is a quality that gathers similar things and separates opposites, the definition of Cold is the same in reverse: it gathers opposites and separates similar things. This occurs so that both difference and harmony may fall upon the essences of opposites equally, even if it causes partial decay. Therefore, O observer, you must search and look into these arduous sciences that are difficult to perceive, and you shall attain your quest. I have only set before you these wonderful points from all the sciences to protect your soul, so that you may search hereafter for these wondrous sciences to attain what the **Sages** <term>Sages: the 'First Sages' usually refers to ancient philosophers like Hermes Trismegistus, Aristotle, or Plato</term> attained—the First Sages who ascended to where they ascended.

->( Chapter on Stones )<-

Among these, **Saturn** governs stones such as iron, diamond, antimony <term>antimony: "athmad," used both as a mineral and as kohl for the eyes</term>, onyx, pure black jet, magnetized stone <term>magnetized stone: "nayruj," likely referring to a specific treated or naturally occurring magnetic mineral</term>, magnetite, and magnesia. He also shares influence over gold, ruby, golden marcasite, and the *sazaj* stone.

->( Jupiter )<-

**Jupiter** governs stones such as tin <note>original: "al-anuk"</note>, white and yellow ruby <note>note: in this context, "ruby" (yaqut) refers to precious corundum, including sapphires</note>. He also shares influence over carnelian, gold, peridot, alabaster, and crystal. He governs every bright, white stone.

->( Mars )<-

**Mars** governs stones such as red copper, the "surin" stone, and all types of sulfur. He shares influence over marcasite, bloodstone, magnetite, glass, carnelian, and onyx. He governs every dark-colored red stone.

->( Mercury )<-

**Mercury** governs stones such as "anklos" <note>likely a corruption of a Greek mineral name</note>, "espadanj," emerald, and quicksilver. He shares influence over peridot, tin, glass, and marble.

->( Venus )<-

**Venus** governs stones such as red copper, lapis lazuli, pearls, seashells, malachite <note>original: "dahnaj"</note>, and coral. She shares influence over silver, alabaster, glass, and "muqtiba."

Having mentioned that, I now begin [describing] the forms of the planets based on what we have stated from the book *The Benefits of Stones* by Utarid the Scribe <note>Utarid is the Arabic name for the planet Mercury, but in this context, it refers to a legendary author of medieval astrological and lapidary works</note>, from the book of Aylus the Wise <note>likely a reference to Apollonius of Tyana, a famed philosopher and occultist known in Arabic traditions as Balinas or Aylus</note>, and from the book *The Interpretation of Spiritual Talismans* translated by Batratas. Upon her head is her radiance. ### ( Concerning the depiction of the Greatest Luminary <term>Greatest Luminary: al-Nir al-A'zam; a common title for the Sun in medieval astronomy.</term> )

On a stone of pure red ruby <term>Ruby: yaqut ahmar</term> is the image of a king sitting on a throne with a crown above his head. In front of him is the image of a raven, and beneath his feet are the letters Q-H-R <note>original: "قهر" (qahr); meaning "Subjugation," "Conquest," or "Irresistible Force."</note>. The bearer of this gem shall overcome all other kings... This stone bestows upon its bearer prestige before everyone who meets or confronts him. ### ( Among its engravings )

For repelling snakes: if one engraves on the gemstone of a ring the image of a snake during the hour of the Sun, while it is at seven degrees of Aquarius, Saturn is at seven degrees of Aries, the Moon is at seven degrees of Scorpio, Jupiter is at two degrees of Sagittarius, and the Tail <term>The Tail: al-Dhanab; the descending lunar node, often considered malefic in astrology</term> is at ten degrees of Sagittarius. ### ( Among its engravings for repelling beetles )

Engrave the following symbol upon it and set it in a gold ring. No beetle shall appear in the place where it is kept, nor shall the bearer ever see one.

### ( Venus, engraved for Venus )

The image should have a human body, the face of a bird, and the feet of an eagle. This image makes its bearer beloved by all people. The engraving of this image must take place during her [Venus's] hour and her exaltation.

At her planetary hour <term>Planetary hour: al-sāʿa; a system where each hour of the day is governed by one of the seven classical planets</term>, [one engraves] three figures joined together. The bearer is granted blessings and prosperity in trade. This is the description of the drawing:

->( And for her, it is engraved in coral )<-

The image of two cats with the image of a mouse between them. This is done during her hour and her ascendant <term>Ascendant: al-ṭāliʿ; the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of the ritual</term> when she [Venus] is positioned within it. This image is specifically for banishing mice from the place where it is kept:

<image-desc>A drawing of two cats with a small mouse between them.</image-desc>

->( And for her, it is engraved during her hour )<-

The bearer of this gemstone will find that flies do not approach any place where he is. This is performed when the ascendant belongs to her:

<image-desc>A drawing of a fly or winged insect with spread wings.</image-desc>

->( And for her, it is engraved in malachite )<-

On its face, the image of a leech, and on the underside of the stone, the image of two leeches, the head of one upon the tail of the other. This image removes leeches from any place if an impression is made with it. This occurs during her hour and her ascendant, and likewise during the engraving of the stone; know this.

->( And for her, it is engraved in a stone )<-

During her hour and her ascendant, the image of a full-figured woman <note>original: "mutajasdina"; referring to a physically well-defined or curvaceous female form</note> with an idol before her. This talisman is also used for love, and it is well-known:

The image of a woman holding a rolled cloth in one of her hands, her body covered in markings resembling fine hair <note>original: "al-zaghab"; often used to describe downy hair or peach fuzz</note>, and in her right hand something resembling an apple. One makes an impression with it in wax to treat the illnesses of children and whatever afflicts them, during her hour and her ascendant.

->( And for her, it is engraved in carnelian )<-

Something resembling the head of a wild donkey with the head of a fly upon it, and on the head is a slight protrusion. This is done during her hour and her ascendant. This stone is used to make an impression in wax for colic <note>original: "al-maghlī"; likely a scribal variant for "al-maghṣ," referring to abdominal pain or cramps</note>, and it has been tested and taken from the *Al-Barrāni* <note>original: "al-Barrānī"; likely a reference to a specific source text or the "outer" (exoteric) teachings of a sage like Apollonius of Tyana</note>.

### (From the same book)

<image-desc>A drawing of a lion walking towards the right with its tail curled upwards. Above its head is the Arabic letter 'Dal' (د) and a small mark.</image-desc>

One should engrave upon the face of an emerald <term>Emerald: zumurrud; often used in talismans for authority and protection</term> the image of a lion and a lion's head. Beneath it, write the letter Alif (أ), and above it, write the letter Dal (د) during its planetary hour and its ascendant <term>Ascendant: al-ṭāliʿ; the zodiacal sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of the ritual</term>. The wearer of this gemstone will be beloved and held in awe by all people, and he shall remain safe from the harms of kings.

### (From another book)

Engrave upon a face of green peridot <term>Peridot: zabarjad; also known as chrysolite, believed to have protective qualities</term> the image of a scorpion during its planetary hour and its ascendant. If a pregnant woman wears this gemstone, her child will be safe from all physical defects. The drawing of the scorpion has been provided previously; therefore, one should take the design from there, excluding the snake.

### (For him, it is engraved)

<image-desc>A drawing of a hand holding a balance scale by its central ring. A bird is perched on the horizontal beam of the scale.</image-desc>

Upon a glass stone, during its hour and its ascendant, engrave a human hand holding a scale. All types of fevers are treated by stamping with this gemstone <note>original: "yaṭbaʿu bi-hādha al-faṣṣ"; implying the ring is used to stamp a seal into a substance to be consumed or worn</note>, for it surely cures them.

### (Requirements for the image of the Moon)

<image-desc>A horizontal sequence of magical sigils and characters: '1 5 1 0 L C' followed by geometric symbols and the Arabic letters 'm' (م) and 'h' (ح).</image-desc>

If the image of a scorpion is engraved upon a bezoar stone <term>Bezoar: bāzahar; a stone-like mass found in the digestive systems of animals, highly prized in medieval medicine as an antidote to poison</term> while the Moon is in Scorpio, during the Moon's hour, and while Scorpio is in one of the cardines <term>Cardines: awtād; the four most powerful houses in an astrological chart (1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th)</term>, and it is set into a gold ring; then, let that ring be used to stamp a seal into chewed frankincense <term>Frankincense: kundur; a resin used here as a medicinal medium</term> while the Moon is in Scorpio. That frankincense is then given as a drink to someone who has been stung by a scorpion. The people have related that this is well-tested, and we have mentioned it before.

### (For him, it is engraved)

Upon a face of lapis lazuli <term>Lapis Lazuli: lāzaward; a deep blue stone associated with the heavens</term>, engrave this design during its hour and its ascendant. If that gemstone is dipped into any kind of drink and then shared by two or more people, affection will grow between them, even if they were previously enemies.

### (For him, from the Book of the Interpretation of Spiritual Talismans)

Translated by Pancratius <note>original: "Baqrāṭīs"; likely a reference to the Greco-Egyptian or Hermetic source material common in Arabic grimoires</note>. If there is engraved upon a gemstone the image of a man with the head of a bird, leaning upon a staff, and before him is a bundle of feverfew <term>Feverfew: uthuwān; a medicinal herb (Tanacetum parthenium) often used for inflammation</term>, during its hour, its ascendant, and its exaltation; it shall assist the wearer in walking. He will not grow weary even if he travels a great distance. The drawing for this was provided previously in this discourse, except that the face there was a human face with the form of a bird above his head, the tree was in front of him, and he was leaning on the staff in his hand—so look for it there.

From another book: if there is engraved upon a crystal stone the image of a woman with her hair forming a right angle, with two bulls beneath her, having placed one foot on the head of one bull and the other foot on the head of the second; and on the inner side of the gemstone is the image of a standing woman with something resembling a crown upon her head and a whip in her right hand. Engrave this with it:

The property of this stone is that it should be used to stamp wax <note>The text suggests creating a seal or amulet by pressing the engraved stone into wax</note>, which is then placed in a domed structure. Pigeons will come to it and multiply in that place until their number becomes countless.

The image of a fly is to be made during the hour of the Moon, when it is in the twenty-first degree <note>original: "kā-daraja"; using Abjad numerals where Kāf=20 and Alif=1</note> of Libra, with Jupiter at two degrees of Sagittarius, and Saturn in the first degree of Capricorn. Draw the first design upon the body of the fly and the second design around it—which is this one shown here. ->(Engravings and images of Saturn)<-

When it is engraved on a bezoar stone <term>Bezoar: bāzahr; a stone-like mass found in the digestive systems of animals, highly prized as a universal antidote and protective charm in medieval lapidaries</term> during his hour while he is in the ascendant at the 21st degree <note>original: "kā-daraja"; using the Abjad system where Kāf=20 and Alif=1</note> of Libra: the image of a man with a large body and a long, respected face, riding a plow with two oxen before him. Around his neck is a necklace containing a human head and a fox head. This image has the effect of protecting farms, cattle, crops, and plantations, and strengthening them. Likewise, it aids in orchards, irrigation channels, the stability of buildings, resisting those who show hostility, dispelling grudges, repelling anxieties, and it prevents the wearer from fearing any person.

During his hour and ascendant, this design.

It is to be stamped into pitch <note>Pitch: al-qār; a thick black resin like tar, used here for disruptive or "dark" magic intended to cause separation</note> to cast enmity and discord between people who love each other, and it is placed in their location. The original seal must be kept protected; its maker should not hold it nor approach it except at the time of stamping with it.

This design—and the action resulting from it—prolongs the life of its possessor and preserves his life. This drawing has already appeared previously in this treatise.

### ### (For him, regarding the expulsion of locusts)

<image-desc>A drawing of a locust or grasshopper in profile, facing right. Above it are magical characters resembling Arabic letters: a symbol like the numeral 4, followed by "K-A-Ay-La".</image-desc>

Engrave on an obsidian stone <term>Obsidian: sabaj; a dark, volcanic glass often used in protective magic</term> the image of a locust during his planetary hour, while he is at two degrees of Aries, Mars is at five degrees of Cancer, and the Moon is at three degrees of Capricorn. With these specific degrees, the talisman <term>Talisman: tilsam; an object inscribed with astronomical and symbolic figures to harness celestial power</term> becomes effective. Draw this diagram around the locust and place rabbit skin beneath it after mounting it. Then, no locusts shall ever enter the place where the talisman is located, whether it is by itself or with its bearer.

### ### (For him, let there be engraved)

<image-desc>A drawing of a rabbit or hare sitting and facing right. Above it is a string of magical symbols and characters, including what looks like numerals and sigils: "18 L-Hh 7 La 36 La L-Hh 99111".</image-desc>

On any stone you wish, engrave the image of a rabbit with Libra in the ascendant, Saturn at the 38th degree <note>original: "l-kh daraja"; This may be a scribal error or a specific calculation, as a zodiac sign contains only 30 degrees</note> of Gemini, and the Sun at the 5th degree of Cancer. Draw the diagram around it, then mount it in a ring and wear it; for the woman whom the wearer seeks will never conceive from him.

### ### (Engravings and images of Jupiter)

<image-desc>A detailed illustration of a central figure wearing a crown and wings, sitting on a throne or chair. The chair is supported by four standing figures whose necks serve as the legs of the chair. Two smaller winged figures (possibly angels) are shown below the chair.</image-desc>

Engrave for him on a white sapphire <term>White sapphire: yāqūt abyad; refers to clear corundum or other precious white stones</term> gemstone the image of a man with a crown upon his head, sitting on a throne with four legs. Each leg rests upon the neck of a standing man, and these men have wings. He has his hand raised in prayer. This must be done during his hour and while he is in his exaltation <term>Exaltation: sharaf; the specific zodiacal position where a planet's influence is at its peak</term>. The effect of this ring for the wearer is to increase his prestige and status, help him acquire wealth, reunite families, and increase and protect his children. It settles matters in the best possible way, completes the righteous deeds the wearer undertakes, and suppresses the <unclear>schemes</unclear> of enemies. Through it, a person succeeds in his endeavors and remains safe from the assassination attempts of his foe.

### ### (For him, let there be engraved)

On a green sapphire <term>Green sapphire: yāqūt akhdar</term> gemstone, engrave the image of a man with the face of a lion and the feet of a bird. Beneath his feet is a three-headed dragon, and in his right hand is a javelin with which he stabs the dragon's heads. This is done during his hour and the first decan <note>original: "al-wajh al-awwal"; the first 10-degree division of a zodiac sign</note> of his house of exaltation. Its effect is to remove enemies for the wearer and grant him awe and grandeur in their presence. This design and its image have already been mentioned previously in this treatise.

### ### (For him, let there be engraved on a crystal stone)

The image of a handsome man wearing a turban, riding an eagle. Its effect is to make you beloved by judges and those who uphold civil laws. The engraving must be done during his hour and exaltation. This design has also been mentioned previously in this treatise.

<meta>This page continues the catalog of planetary talismans, transitioning from the final attributes of Jupiter to the aggressive and protective engravings associated with Mars. ### ### (For him, let there be engraved)

On the stone known as the "Stone of the Beginning of Birth" <term>Stone of the Beginning of Birth: Hajar mustahal al-wilada; historically identified as the Aetites or "Eagle-stone," a geode believed to have protective properties for pregnancy</term>, engrave the image of an eagle during his [Jupiter's] planetary hour, with the first face <term>Face: A subdivision of a zodiac sign, also known as a decan, consisting of ten degrees</term> of Sagittarius in the ascendant. The wearer of this engraving, if he goes out to a place where birds dwell intending to catch them, they shall gather around him and never leave him. Its bearer will also be accepted and beloved among people. This stone is one that tends toward redness; inside it is a moving stone that rattles when shaken, and if rubbed, a white liquid emerges from it. Its specific property is that if a woman hangs it upon herself, she shall never conceive. The drawing of the eagle has already been mentioned, depicting Jupiter riding upon it with a spear or rod in his hand, wearing a cloak <note>original: "burda"; a traditional striped or outer garment</note> in this treatise.

### ### (Hermes mentioned)

<image-desc>A simplified drawing of a four-legged animal, likely a fox (thalab), walking towards the right with a bushy tail.</image-desc>

That this stone is blessed among stones. Whoever takes it and engraves upon it the image of a fox on the day of Jupiter and during his planetary hour, while the Moon is looking at him [aspecting Jupiter] and Jupiter is in Pisces, shall experience its effect: if a person carries it with them, both Jinn and humans will be struck with awe and fear of them.

### ### (For him, let there be engraved)

<image-desc>A profile drawing of a bird, identified in the text as a nightingale (bulbul), facing right.</image-desc>

On this stone, the image of a nightingale during the hour of Jupiter while he is in the house of his exaltation <note>Jupiter's exaltation is in Cancer</note>. Wash that stone and give the water to someone to drink; they will then see spiritual beings <term>Spiritual beings: Ruhaniya; the spirits or essences associated with planets or higher realms</term> and may employ them as they wish. This was also mentioned by Hermes, containing whatever signs and hints he intended.

### ### (Engravings and images of Mars)

Engrave upon a magnetite stone <term>Magnetite: Hajar maghnitis; a naturally magnetized mineral</term> the image of a man riding a lion, holding a drawn sword in his right hand and a human head in his left, during his [Mars'] hour while he is in the second face of Aries. It performs wonders in both evil and good, though its work in evil is greater. Its drawing has already been mentioned in this treatise.

### ### (And for him, from the engravings)

On one of the stones of Mars, engrave the image of a standing man wearing armor, girded with two swords—one of them drawn in his hand—and holding a human head in his left hand during his hour. The wearer of this engraving will find that majesty and power <note>original: "al-mahaba wa al-satwa"</note> are bestowed upon him in the eyes of whoever sees him or associates with him. I have seen this exact engraving on a carnelian <term>Carnelian: 'Aqiq; a reddish-brown semi-precious stone</term> seal-ring belonging to one of the practitioners of this path. Its drawing has already been mentioned in this treatise.

- ٧٠ -

Talismans are also fashioned for these planets, possessing wondrous effects according to specific proportions:

**(The Sun)** during its hour and the first face <term>Face: A subdivision of a zodiac sign, also known as a decan, consisting of ten degrees</term> of Leo: a talisman that removes disturbances of the wicked soul and stills pains of the liver and stomach, as well as difficult illnesses.

**(For the Moon)** during the hour of the first face of Cancer: a talisman for the growth of crops, trees, and all varieties of plants.

**(For Saturn)** during its hour and the third face of Aquarius: a talisman that cures urinary incontinence and halts hemorrhaging in women.

**(For Jupiter)** in the second face of Sagittarius with the Sun aspecting it: a talisman to cease excessive rain and the fear of flooding.

**(For Mars)** during its hour while in the first face of Scorpio: a talisman that emboldens the coward, breaks the wrath of the ruler, returns the runaway to his place, and wards off the harm of thieves, predatory beasts, wolves, and every source of evil.

**(For Venus)** during its hour while in the first face of Pisces: a talisman that heals women of uterine pain, soothes the wicked soul afflicted by melancholy <note>original: "al-manukhalia"; a term derived from the Greek *melancholia*, used here to describe a state of deep despondency or mental disturbance</note>, gladdens the heart, and aids in increasing sexual vigor.

**(In the first face of Taurus)**: a talisman to inspire love and perform noble deeds.

**(For Mercury)** during its hour while in the first face of Gemini: a talisman that purifies the mind, stimulates thought, encourages the pursuit of sciences, and is used for achieving status among people and similar aims.

### (Section)

The ancients who specialized in this matter have spoken so much on this subject that if we were to gather all their sayings and schools of thought, this book would become exceedingly long. However, our purpose is brevity—focusing only on what is necessary and what no student or researcher of this craft can do without.

Let your gaze, oh observer, be for your own soul’s sake. Whenever a part of this art is revealed to you, do not disclose it to others. Know that the people who find the most pleasure in the sciences are those who trade in them for a living, but the one who truly engages with them and contemplates them does so to become an elite <term>Elite: *Khassa*; those with specialized knowledge, as opposed to the *'Amma* or general public</term>, distinguishing himself from the common folk—many of whom do not realize the difficulty of this science's aim.

The astrologer is sometimes asked for things beyond the power of the heavens; how then can they be within the power of the astrologer? If he succeeds, people do not praise him for his accuracy, and if he fails, his status diminishes in their eyes. Therefore, you must not tear away the veil for the common people. These spiritual sciences were only grasped by the sages through immense hardship and toil. They reached their conclusions only after much labor, research, and excellence of mind. For it is through mental excellence that one gains the ability to recognize the truth of a sage's words amidst conflicting opinions, and the power to verify it. This is the excellence of deduction—the ability to extract what is true from various views. In essence, it is a form of intellect; it is the purity of the rational soul <term>Rational Soul: *al-nafs al-natiqa*; in Islamic philosophy, the highest part of the human soul capable of reason</term> combined with a sharpened imaginative faculty, clear pathways of thought, and a swift acceptance of its goals. God knows best what is right, and to Him is the return and final destination.

The treatise *The Goal of the Wise* <term>Goal of the Wise: *Ghayat al-Hakim*; a famous 10th-century Arabic grimoire of astral magic, later translated into Latin as the *Picatrix*</term> by the master Al-Majriti <note>original: "al-Majriti"; Abu al-Qasim al-Majriti (d. c. 1007), a mathematician and astronomer from al-Andalus</note> is completed by the grace of God, as per my promise to my dear brothers. I praise God the Almighty for His guidance, and I ask Him to lead us all to the straight path of goodness and reform. I have previously mentioned to your excellency, in the first part of the book *The Essence of Revelations in the Science of Letters*, some methods for treating metals. To complete the benefit, I now transcribe for you another gift from the great master and scholar Sheikh Al-Ghamri (may God have mercy on him), which is *The Unveiling of Secrets in Solving the Mystery of Treasures*. I have concluded this treatise with other sublime benefits. I say, and with God is success.

# (Revealing the Symbols in Solving the Treasures)

->In the name of God, the Gracious, the Merciful<-

Know, O reader, that the ancient sages and the early philosophers—just as they understood the Stone and the Science of the Balance <note>The "Science of the Balance" (Ilm al-Mizan) is a core concept in Jabirian alchemy, referring to the precise proportions of qualities—hot, cold, dry, and moist—within substances</note> to be included among the seven metallic bodies—fashioned from them gold, silver, emeralds, rubies, and various types of jewels. They then placed these in their treasure hoards. You will often find silver in their stores because silver that has been treated with the elixir and produced through the Science of the Balance does not decay or turn green in the earth, even if it were to remain there until the Day of Resurrection. Furthermore, when they established those places and created those riches, they required guards for those hoards. They found that within the Stone and its varieties, God—Glory be to Him—has deposited all the medicinal properties found in all generated things, as well as all the harms of poisons, all benefits, and all the celestial observations and "lower" and "upper" spiritual workings. Thus, they crafted talismans to preserve their bodies, their wealth, and their hoards. Know that every metallic body is attributed to one of the planets; the metal is like a child to it, and the planet is like the father from whom it draws its movements, its rays, and its influence.

## (The Preparation of Lead)

The first thing the sages focused on was the body of Lead, which is *al-Asrub* <note>original: "al-Asrub"; an archaic term for lead used in alchemical texts</note>, because it is the first of the metallic bodies, their father, and the one that precedes them. In it lies the Greatest Secret. They took from the salt specifically associated with it—to ensure a swift result—which is "Natron salt." They dissolved it in pure water <term>Pure water: "al-Ma' al-Qarah," refers to distilled water or clear, unadulterated water</term>, filtered it, and solidified it once. Then they crushed it and divided it into two halves after calcination <note>Calcination is the process of heating a metal or mineral to high temperatures to reduce it to a powder or "calx"</note>. They dissolved one of the halves in pure water and mixed it with an equal weight of olive oil (meaning equal parts of the calcined powder, water, and oil). They cooked this over a gentle fire, and it instantly solidified into a soap as white as snow. The sages call this "Saturnine Soap" <note>Saturn is traditionally the planetary ruler of Lead</note>. They then rotated the Lead in a crucible and "fed" it a small amount of the calcined Natron, then poured it into some of that soap, repeating this seven times. It thus transformed into a pure, clean, and solid lead that will not change until the Day of Resurrection and will not be consumed by the earth. Among the sages, it is known as "Pure Lead" and "The Gold That Was," because through this purification, it transitioned into a new quality and essence, and the spirituality of Saturn was raised from it. The goal of these folk was nothing other than to manifest spirituality within their treasures. They then fashioned a tablet from it and engraved on it the image of the degree of the ascendant—which is Aquarius and Capricorn—during the hour of the Lord of the Ascendant, which is the planet of this metal [Saturn]. They then fumigated it with the incense of that planet and buried it in the treasure in the direction of the *Qibla* <note>The direction of Mecca, here signifying the South</note>, which is the southern quarter. From it arose the spirituality of Saturn: these are kings and helpers appearing in the form of the kings of Abyssinia and Sudan riding upon elephants. There also appear in that treasure buffaloes, *namus* <note>original: "al-namus"; likely referring to spiritual laws or winged spirits, or perhaps gnats/insects in a literal sense</note>, old women, old men, monks, scorpions, snakes, and beetles. If the sage sees any of these, he knows they are from the spirituality of Saturn and nullifies them with his wisdom. As for the ignorant man, he perishes; so understand these secrets.

## (The Preparation of Tin)

Then they focused on *al-Qal’i* lead <note>original: "al-Rasas al-Qal'i"; a term for Tin, associated with the planet Jupiter</note>. They found that the mineral salt within it, which is linked to its spirituality, is *al-Andarani* <note>Andarani salt: a high-quality crystalline rock salt frequently used in medieval chemistry</note>. They dissolved it in pure water, extracted it, and solidified it once. Then they [processed] it and divided it into two halves after calcination. They dissolved the first half in an equal amount of pure water, then mixed it with an equal weight of olive oil and cooked it, for it solidifies instantly into a soap like snow, and this...

This soap is called by them "The Happy Soap" and "The Greatest Fortune." Then they rotated the Tin <note>original: "al-Qal'i," referring to high-quality tin, likely from Qala in the Malay Peninsula</note> and fed it with some of the calcined half, then poured it into some of this soap, repeating this seven times until it becomes a pure Tin that is not altered by the passage of ages nor the depths of the earth.

Know that this pure Tin enters into the Science of the Balance <note>The "Science of the Balance" refers to the precise mathematical proportion of qualities—hot, cold, moist, and dry—within a substance</note> and the elixir is cast upon it. Then they fashioned from it a tablet during the rising of Sagittarius or Pisces, in the hour of the planet [Jupiter]. Then they fumigated it with the incense of that planet after engraving upon it the image of the rising degree. Finally, they buried it in the western quarter of that treasure; from this is generated the spiritual essence <note>original: "Ruhaniyya," a spiritual entity or "intelligence" associated with a planet</note> that stands as the guardian of Jupiter, and they appear in the likeness of judges.

### (The Preparation of Iron)

Then they turned their attention to the body of Iron. They found that the mineral salt attributed to it is "Common Salt" [table salt]. They dissolved it in pure water, stirred it, and solidified it once. Then they calcined <note>Calcination involves heating a substance to high temperatures to reduce it to a powder or "calx"</note> it and divided it into two halves. One half they dissolved in sea water—equal to the weight of the half—then they cast upon it an equal weight of oil and cooked it until it became a "relaxing soap." They then coated sheets of iron with some of that soap and rolled the sheet in the crushed, calcined salt. Then they heated the sheet and quenched it in some of that soap, repeating this seven times until it became a pure Iron that does not change through the passage of time nor is it eaten by the depths of the earth.

Then they fashioned from it a tablet during the rising of Aries or Scorpio in the hour of the lord of the rising sign [Mars]. Then they engraved upon it the rising degree—that is, its image—and fumigated it with the incense of Mars. They buried it in the eastern direction, and there arose from it the spiritual essence of Mars. These are assistants in the likeness of Turkish kings <note>In medieval Arabic texts, different ethnicities were often used to describe the appearance of planetary spirits; "Turkish" often implied martial or warrior-like appearances</note> and Turkish helpers with various types of weapons. From it also arise calamities, terrifying fires, and the like.

### (The Preparation of Gold)

Then they turned their attention to the body of Gold. They found the specific mineral salt for it is Saltpeter <note>original: "Malh al-Barud"</note>. Then they dissolved it in an equal amount of water without filtration or calcination, because it is already a pure spiritual substance. Then they cooked it with oil, and it became a soap like snow. They then coated sheets of gold with some of the soap and rolled them in the saltpeter. Then they heated it and quenched it in some of the oil—that is, the soap—doing this seven times. Thus it became the "Talismanic Gold" of the People; it is pure by its very nature, but the gold of the common people was not suitable for the talismans of the Sages.

Then they fashioned from it a tablet during the rising of Leo, in the hour of the lord of that rising sign, which is the Sun. They then engraved the image of the rising degree of Leo at that time, fumigated it with the incense of that planet, and buried it inside the treasure in the eastern quarter. From it arose the spiritual essence of the Sun, which takes the form of Persian kings <note>original: "Muluk al-Ajam"</note> and Indian kings. From it also appear terrifying lions, deadly leopards, cats, eagles, and the like. This is not hidden from the Knower; for when the Knower sees any obstacle, he knows which planet is ruling over it, and after that, he can work against it to nullify it.

### (The Preparation of Copper)

Then, after that, they took the body of Copper and took the mineral salt suitable for it, which is Vitriol salt <note>original: "Malh al-Zaj"</note>. They dissolved it in pure water, then filtered it and solidified it once. Then they calcined it until it became red like a ruby. They divided it into two halves and dissolved one half in an equal amount of pure water, then mixed it with an equal amount of oil and cooked it until it became a "golden soap." Then they coated sheets of copper with some of that soap and rolled it in the calcined vitriol. Then they heated it and quenched it in some of that soap, repeating this seven times. It thus becomes a pure copper that is not changed by the ages or the depths of the earth, and it does not corrode <note>original: "yuzanjir," to turn green with verdigris</note>. This pure copper enters into the Science of the Balance and accepts [the elixir]...

It details how these prepared metals are used to summon specific spiritual guardians (ranging from judges to warrior-kings and wild beasts) to protect hidden treasures.</summary>

the elixir-like instruments, for it is copper washed with soap <note>In alchemical texts, "soap" (sabun) refers to a chemical cleansing agent or a prepared reagent used to "fix" or purify metals, rather than household soap.</note>. Then they fashioned from it a tablet during the rising of Taurus or Libra, in the hour of the Lord of the Rising <note>The "Lord of the Rising" is the planet that rules the zodiac sign appearing on the eastern horizon at a specific moment.</note>. Then they engraved upon that tablet the specific degree that was rising at that time from that zodiacal sign, and they fumigated it with the incense of Venus. From it also arises the "Bride of the Treasure"; when the seeker sees her, he is struck with awe and bewilderment by her beauty. She approaches him laughing, and as he stands stunned, she embraces him and her breasts pierce through his back, and he dies instantly. Understand these matters and these traps.

### (The Preparation of Mercury)

Then they took Mercury and solidified it with the scent of Lead <note>original: "al-Asrub," an old term for lead</note>, so it solidified into a melted, enduring body, yet it remained brittle and would not accept the spirit <note>In alchemy, "accepting the spirit" refers to the metal's ability to bond or transmute further</note>. Then they took the salt attributed to it, which is Sal Ammoniac <note>original: "al-Nashadur," ammonium chloride, essential in medieval chemistry for volatile reactions</note>. They dissolved it in an equal amount of pure water without the processes of dissolution, solidification, or calcination, for it is spiritual, like gunpowder. Then they mixed it with oil and cooked it with the body into a "Mercurial Soap." Then they melted this solidified body [of Mercury] and fed it with a little Sal Ammoniac, then poured it into some of that soap. They repeated this 7 times until it became a solid body that does not crumble. From this, they fashioned a tablet and engraved upon it, during the rising of Gemini or Virgo, the image of the degree rising at that time. They fumigated it with the incense of that planet [Mercury] and buried it. From it arose the spiritual entities corresponding to that planet: a people in the likeness of scribes, and beasts in the likeness of dogs, crows, rabbits, and the like.

### (The Preparation of the Moon)

Then, after that, they took the body of the Moon <note>The "body of the Moon" is the alchemical term for Silver.</note> and took the salt attributed to it, which is Yemenite Alum. They dissolved it in pure water, processed it, solidified it, and calcined it. Then they made from it, as previously described, a "Lunar Soap." Then they anointed the silver plates with it and rubbed them in the calcined alum. They heated it and quenched it in the soap seven times. Then they fashioned a tablet from it and engraved upon it—during the rising of Cancer in the hour of the Moon—the image of the degree rising at that time from the sign of Cancer. Then they fumigated it with the incense of the planet and buried it in the location, in the maritime [Northern] quarter. The same applies to Mercury mentioned previously. From it arise the spiritualities and talismans corresponding to the Moon, such as salt seas that have no shore, fresh seas, rain, snow, clouds, and thunder. Guardians also appear in the likeness of ministers, the bearers of torches, and other things attributed to the Moon.

> Understand these secrets which I have revealed to you, for they are never to be found in any other book like this. These secrets have been hidden since the time of Adam, peace be upon him, among the things concealed by the Sages.

### (Then, after that, they fashioned the Greatest Talisman)

This talisman is called the "Solomonic Talisman," the "Seal of Solomon" (peace be upon him), and the "Greatest Rising." The Sages have mentioned it in symbols; Jabir <note>referring to Jabir ibn Hayyan, the famous 8th-century polymath and "father of chemistry"</note> alluded to it in the *Book of Balances*. The "Author of Vows" also alluded to it at the beginning of his collection when mentioning the seven planets, and the Sages called it the "Seven Idols." Al-Jildaki <note>A 14th-century alchemist known for his extensive commentaries on earlier works</note> mentioned it in *The Proof* (al-Burhan), as did the author of *The Rising Places of the Full Moons* (Matali' al-Budur).

They combine the seven bodies <note>The seven metals: Gold, Silver, Mercury, Copper, Iron, Tin, and Lead</note> in the casting process according to specific conditions. They use "Chinese Iron" <note>original: "Khar Sini," likely referring to zinc or a specific white alloy like paktong</note> in place of Mercury, for it is attributed to it. Then they gather the seven mentioned salts attributed to them and make them into a single soap; some have named this soap "The Great Borax."

Then they rotate the bodies <note>Metals</note> and purify them seven times as previously mentioned, and from them, they fashion a tablet. This is called the **Tablet of Obedience**, the **Seal of Subjugation**, and the **Seven-Faced Bead of Kosan son of Canaan** <note>Kosan (or Kawsan) ibn Kan'an is a figure often appearing in medieval Arabic grimoires as a master of talismans</note>. They then engrave upon it the number of the seven forms, which are the degrees of the rising signs mentioned for various times. Understand these secrets, and you shall become a realized master in your time among your brothers. And God knows best.

## (Another Chapter) On Sulfur Oil and Its Properties

Know, O seeker, that the science of medicine is truly a noble science, yet its success is hidden. The physicians who laid down its foundations—such as **Luqman** <note>A legendary sage often identified with Aesop or a pre-Islamic wise man</note>, **Ibn Sina** <note>Known in the West as Avicenna (980–1037 AD)</note>, **Galen**, **Dawud al-Antaki** <note>A 16th-century blind physician from Antioch known for his "Memorial"</note>, and other honorable physicians—produced medicines that were successful against all diseases and ailments. Their works never failed. They left us their books, yet through experimentation, we find they often no longer provide benefit. This is because the core utility has been lost from that science—specifically, the oil of **"The Persian Fire"** <note>original: "al-Nar al-Farisiya"; likely referring to a specific alchemical preparation of sulfur</note>. The success of medical science depends upon it; it enters into all its materials, and without it, the science of medicine offers nothing to the seeker. Because Luqman, Dawud, Ibn Sina, **al-Razi** <note>Rhazes (865–925 AD), a pioneer of clinical medicine</note>, and others utilized this Persian oil in all their works, their treatments were superior. When this oil was lost from their books, the success of medicine diminished. Therefore, I wished to mention it to complete the benefit. I say, and with God is success:

**(Know)** that this oil enters into every medical remedy for every disease, as will be explained.

**(This is the method of its manufacture):** Take sulfur, grind it, and sift it. Pour sharp vinegar over it seven times <note>The process involves repeatedly dousing and drying</note>, then rotate it and fry it in fresh milk seven times. Then grind it and mix it with a third of its weight of **Sal Ammoniac** <term>Sal Ammoniac: Ammonium chloride, used as a flux in alchemy to help substances dissolve or react</term>. Knead it with lemon water into a dry dough, place it in a glass bottle, and submerge it in a **vinegar well** <note>A pit containing fermenting vinegar or dung to provide a steady, mild heat</note> for a day and a night. It will dissolve into a white liquid with the consistency of milk. Add to it a third of its weight of rose water and place it over a gentle fire until it blends. Keep it in a glass bottle; this is the **Persian Sulfur Oil**. We shall mention some of its uses in treating diseases.

### (Treatment of Madness and Melancholy)

This is a wicked disease that has exhausted physicians. When you wish to treat it, take one part fat from a dead horse and one part of the aforementioned oil. Mix them over a gentle fire. The patient should take one <term>dirham</term> <note>A unit of weight roughly equal to 3 grams</note> as snuff <note>inhaled through the nose</note> at bedtime and another in the morning. Also, rub his head with the weight of two dirhams after removing his hair. Continue this for three days, and he shall be cured by the permission of God Almighty, even if he had been mad for thirty years.

**(Treatment of Favus)** <note>original: "al-Qara'"; a fungal infection of the scalp resulting in crusting and hair loss</note>, which has exhausted physicians: Take **white lead** <term>Isfidaj: Basic lead carbonate, a common historical pigment and topical treatment</term> and burnt date pits, both finely ground and kneaded with this aforementioned oil. Apply it to the head of the sufferer after cleaning away the crusts. Apply this paste only once, and he will be healed by God's permission. This paste also removes unknown skin eruptions and **ringworm** <note>original: "al-quba"</note>. It also treats fire burns and yellowing <note>jaundice-like skin discoloration</note>, which will heal in two days.

**(Treatment of Scabies)** <note>original: "al-jarab"</note>: Apply this oil directly without adding anything to it, and the patient will be cured from a single application, by the permission of God Almighty.

**(Treatment of Excessive Headache):** Take **black seed** <note>Nigella sativa</note>, **galangal** <note>original: "al-khulanjan"; a ginger-like root</note>, **mastic** <note>a resin from the Pistacia lentiscus tree</note>, and **sal ammoniac**. Roast them all, grind them, and knead them with this Persian oil. Dry the mixture and grind it again after drying until it is like dust. Whoever inhales this as snuff will find the headache, arising from any cause, gone instantly by God's permission.

**(Treatment of Insomnia):** Take the "flower of the one who does not sleep." Grind **fava beans** <note>original: "al-baqilla"</note> finely and knead them with this Persian oil. Let the patient eat three [doses], and he will sleep as he used to, even if he were mad.

<meta>This page continues the transition from the theoretical foundations of medicine to practical applications using the "Persian Oil" (or Persian Sulfur Oil) introduced at the end of the previous page. The text provides a series of specific remedies for neurological, dermatological, and sensory ailments, centering on this specific alchemical preparation as the primary active ingredient.</meta>

**(Treatment for Excessive Sleep)** for the person who sleeps too much. Take the fruit <note>Likely referring to a specific medicinal fruit or seed mentioned in the preceding context</note>, crush it, and knead it with this oil. The patient should eat one **dirham** <note>A unit of weight roughly equal to 3.2 grams</note> of it at bedtime for three days and continue this practice; their sleep will become balanced and light.

**(Treatment for Melasma and Freckles)** on the face. Take radish seeds, **cinnamon** <note>original: "dar sini," literally "Chinese wood"</note>, and mahaleb cherry seeds. Crush them all, knead them with this **Persian Oil**, and apply it to the skin; it will be healed by the will of God Almighty.

**(Treatment for the Ears)** for swelling and similar issues like discharge, pus, and the like. Take one part **ladanum frankincense** <note>A resinous gum from Cistus shrubs</note> and one part of the oil, and knead them with egg white into a soft paste. Drop some into the ear, and it will be healed by the will of God Almighty from a single application. This is among the proven treatments for the flow of blood, pus, and fluid from the ear, as well as severe pain and strong swelling: Take onion water, **sarcocolla** <note>original: "anzarut," a medicinal resin from trees in the Middle East used to heal wounds</note>, gallnuts, and the **Persian Oil** in equal parts. Knead them with fine olive oil into a soft paste and drop it into the ear at bedtime; it will be healed by the will of God Almighty after two applications.

**(Treatment for Deafness and Hearing Loss)** which has exhausted physicians, and for all other ailments specific to the ear. Take apricot kernels, peach kernels, and onion seeds. Crush them all and invert them <note>This likely refers to a "descensory" distillation where the ingredients are heated and the oil is collected as it drips downward</note> until a red oil descends from them. Take one part of this and one part of the **Persian Oil**; blend them over a fire until they are thoroughly mixed. Drop this into the ear, and it will heal deafness, hearing loss, and every disease in the ear in one or two applications.

**(Treatment for Ophthalmia)** <note>original: "ramad," an inflammatory eye condition often identified as conjunctivitis or trachoma</note>. Soak sumac in rose water until the water turns red. Take one part of the water and an equal part of the **Persian Oil**. It will soothe the pain, heal the eye, and cure the ophthalmia.

**(Treatment for Redness in the Eye)**. Dissolve tamarind in water and take one part of it and one part of the aforementioned oil. Blend them over a fire and drop the mixture into the eye; one shall wake up cured by the will of God Almighty.

**(Treatment for Whiteness in the Eye)** which is a "white water" <note>An archaic term for cataracts, believed in humor theory to be fluid descending from the brain</note> that descends from the brain into the eye. Take **rasakhtah** <note>Red zinc oxide or "red tutia"</note> and quench it in vinegar seven times until it becomes calcined. Wash it with hot water and salt until it resembles gold filings. Crush it with an equal amount of **sal ammoniac** <note>original: "nushadar," ammonium chloride</note> and knead it with sharp vinegar into a soft paste. Place it in a glass vessel and put it into a "vinegar well" <note>A pit used to maintain a constant temperature for alchemical digestion</note> for a day and a night, until it dissolves into a thick red liquid. Take one part of this and one part of the **Persian Oil** and drop it into the eye in the morning for three days; it will be healed without a doubt by the will of God Almighty. This medicine removes both "white water" <note>cataracts</note> and "black water" <note>glaucoma</note> from the eye by the will of God Almighty.

**(Treatment for Cloudiness)** where one cannot see at night. Take **sandarac** <note>original: "sandarus," a resin from the Barbary thuja tree</note>, crush it, and knead it with the **Persian Oil**. Dry it and crush it into a fine powder. Using this as **kohl** <note>An eye-liner used for both cosmetic and medicinal purposes</note> removes the cloudiness in a single application.

**(Treatment for Weak Vision)** for the one who cannot see or look at fine things. Take a black eggplant and split it into four. Tie the eggplant and hang it in the shade until it dries, having already placed pieces of **tutia** <note>Zinc oxide</note> inside it. Then remove the tutia after a period of four weeks—which is the time required for the eggplant to dry out. After that, take the pieces of tutia and wash them with hot water and salt, as previously mentioned, until they become like filings. Crush them well, knead them with the **Persian Oil**, dry them, and crush them fine. Whoever applies this as kohl for three days or three times will see the stars during the day and will see at night as clearly as he sees during the day.

<meta>This page continues the specific medical recipes utilizing the "Persian Oil" introduced previously. The text moves from treating external sensory organs (ears) to ophthalmology (eyes), respiratory issues, and internal ailments, concluding with an endorsement of the oil's lineage from the sage Luqman.</meta>

<page-num>76</page-num>

->** (Treatment for Inverted Eyelashes and Pannus) **<-
This refers to red veins that appear over the white and the pupil of the eye. Take eggshells and burnt camel hair; grind them together and knead them with **Persian Sulfur Oil** <note>original: "dahna al-kibrit al-farsiyya," an alchemical preparation of sulfur and oil</note>. Dry the mixture and grind it into a fine powder. Using it as a kohl <note>an eye-powder applied with a stylus</note> even once will remove the problematic eyelash after it has caused irritation and will clear the pannus <note>original: "al-subul," a condition where blood vessels grow into the cornea</note>.

->** (Treatment for Excessive Tearing and Congestion) **<-
Grind **antimony** <note>original: "ithmid," the traditional mineral used for kohl</note> and sandalwood well, knead them with the Persian oil, dry them, and grind them fine. Applying it as kohl just once will heal the eye by the will of God Almighty.

->** (Treatment for Pterygium in the Eye) **<-
This also treats yellowing of the eye, itching, mange, and lesions. Take **cuttlefish bone** <note>original: "zabad al-bahr," literally "sea foam"</note>, salt, and **Cypriot vitriol** <note>original: "zaj qubrusi," likely copper sulfate</note>. Grind all of these finely. Applying it as kohl twice heals all the aforementioned conditions by the will of God Almighty.

->** (Treatment for External and Internal Eye Sores and Strabismus) **<-
Take **red tutty** <note>original: "tutiya hamra," an impure form of zinc oxide used in traditional eye medicine</note> and grind it. Knead it with the milk of a female donkey and add an equal amount of the Persian oil. Dry it and grind it fine. Applying it as kohl twice removes the conditions we mentioned, including strabismus <note>original: "al-hawl," or being cross-eyed</note>.

->** (Treatment for Cloudiness, Spots, Fog, and Mists) **<-
Take red tutty, **sal ammoniac** <note>original: "nushadir," ammonium chloride</note>, rock candy, and **Iraqi verdigris** <note>original: "jinzarah," a green pigment/medicine made from copper</note>. Grind them all and knead them with the Persian oil; dry them and grind them again. Applying it as kohl three times removes what we have described.

->** (Treatment for All Catarrhal Congestions) **<-
Whether located in the hands, the feet, the head, or the eye. Take cloves and **black seed** <note>Nigella sativa</note>. Grind them and knead them with the Persian oil into a dry paste. Then take an equal weight of fine olive oil, heat it over a fire, and add the Persian oil until it melts and becomes a thick oil. Whoever rubs this on the site of the congestion <note>original: "al-nazla," referring to the "downward flow" of humors causing pain or swelling</note> will be healed by the will of God Almighty, and the disease shall not return.

->** (Treatment for Nosebleeds) **<-
This refers to blood descending from the nose or mouth. Take eggshells, grind them, and knead them with the Persian oil. Then take a weight equal to the medicine in white vinegar, place it on the fire, and add the medicine until it melts. Afterward, let it cool and grind it. Whoever inhales it will have their nosebleed stopped instantly. Whoever rinses their mouth with it will stop blood coming from the mouth immediately. Likewise, for a woman with a hemorrhage, if she drinks a draught of it, her bleeding will be prevented instantly and will not return.

->** (Treatment for Coughs of Any Cause) **<-
Take **gum arabic** and white **tragacanth** <note>original: "kathira," a natural gum obtained from dried sap</note>. Grind them, knead them with the Persian oil, and boil them in an equal weight of local rose water until they melt and become a thick liquid. Whoever drinks six **dirhams** <note>a unit of weight, approx. 3.2 grams</note> of it, their cough will be gone. The dose should be taken at bedtime and in the morning for three days; they will be healed by the will of God Almighty, even if the person has suffered from the cough for twenty years.

->** (Treatment for All Internal Diseases) **<-
Take **wild rue** <note>original: "harmal"</note>, garden cress seeds, fennel, **male frankincense** <note>high-quality, tear-shaped resin</note>, mahaleb cherry, and mastic. Grind all of these and knead them with an equal amount of the Persian oil. Then cook it with honey until it becomes an electuary <note>a medicinal paste</note>. Whoever eats one dirham of it for three days at bedtime and in the morning will be healed of all internal diseases, such as **hemiplegia** <note>original: "falij," or half-body paralysis</note>, tumors, **colic** <note>original: "qulanj"</note>, bloating, diarrhea, stomach weakness, and every malady of the interior.

Know that the entire foundation of the science of medicine is this Persian Oil. it is from the remnants of the **Wisdom of Luqman** <note>a legendary sage and physician mentioned in the Quran, often credited with medical discoveries</note>. When you compound any medicine from the science of physic, you must include this oil in it; without it, no medicine will succeed. Know this, and I charge you to keep it hidden from those who are not worthy of it. It is finished, and praise be to God, the Lord of the Worlds. (We shall begin, with God’s help, by recording some beneficial **spiritual benefits** <note>original: "fawa'id ruhaniyya," referring to esoteric, talismanic, or occult knowledge</note>).

### ### (The Invocation of the Shaking of the Abyss) <note>original: "Da'wat Khalhalat al-Huwwa." This is a ritual prayer used to summon or command spiritual forces.</note> also known as the "Shaking of the Winds," with its explanation, seals, and applications, in its complete and perfect form. *

**(Chapter One)** If you desire love, write the first seal <term>khatam: a talismanic design or magic square used to house spiritual power</term> on gazelle parchment <note>the skin of a gazelle, traditionally used for high-stakes or permanent talismans</note> and recite the invocation seven times. Say: "O servants of these names, be entrusted with the love of [Name] and [Name]." Hang it in the air; for the target of the work will not be able to delay and will come to you obediently and by choice.

**(The Second Application)** If you desire love and attraction, write the seven seals for him on a piece of unbaked clay and recite the invocation over it three times. Say: "O servants of these names, be entrusted with bringing [Name] and [Name]," and place it in a hot fire. Before you finish three times, the requested person will have appeared before you by the grace of God.

**(The Third Application)** Take a personal effect <note>original: "athar," an object that has been in contact with the target, like a piece of clothing or hair</note> from the desired person and write the first, second, and third seals upon it. Turn it into a wick and place it in a new lamp. Light it with good oil and tar, and recite the invocation five times. Your work will not be finished before the requested person has appeared before you obediently by the grace of God.

**(Chapter Four)** If you desire this for the bringing of sustenance <note>original: "rizq," referring to wealth, food, or general prosperity</note>, write the seven seals on seven leaves of the Sidr tree—that is, the <term>jujube: the Ziziphus lotus or Christ's Thorn tree, often considered sacred in Islamic tradition</term>. Dry them, crumble them, and sprinkle them in the place you desire. You shall see wonders of blessing and goodness. Recite the invocation over them seven times.

**(Chapter Five)** If you desire this to reveal lost or stolen items, write the seven seals on seven morsels of bread and feed them to the suspected thief; he will be unable to swallow them. Recite the invocation over them seven times, and you shall see a wonder.

**(Chapter Six)** For a headache, if you desire this, write the seven seals on a piece of paper <note>original: "kaghad," a Persian-derived word for paper</note> and hang it upon the head after reciting the invocation over it seven times. He shall be healed by the will of God.

**(Chapter Seven)** To remove a <term>throbbing migraine: original "al-sarib," likely referring to a piercing or pulsating vascular headache</term>, if you desire this, write the first seal on sycamore wood. Recite the invocation without a fixed count while holding a nail in your hand. Place the nail on the first square [of the seal], then move the nail to the second square, and so on, until the pain subsides. In whichever square the pain stops, hammer the nail into it—meaning into the aforementioned wood—and cast it into the sun. The pain will leave him by the will of God Almighty.

**(Chapter Eight)** To remove <term>ophthalmia: original "al-ramad," an inflammatory eye condition</term> from the eye, if you desire this, write the seven seals on olive leaves. Dissolve [the writing] in water and give it to the patient to drink after reading the invocation once. He shall be healed by the will of God Almighty.

**(Chapter Nine)** To remove <term>hemicrania: original "al-shaqiqa," a headache affecting one side of the head</term> from the eye, if you desire this, write the seven seals on the collar of a white shirt and hang it over the place of the pain after reading the invocation three times. He shall be healed by the will of God Almighty.

**(Chapter Ten)** To stop [menstrual] blood, if you desire this, write the first seal on the first day of the month on paper, and she shall carry it with her. As for the remaining seals, write one seal each day in a white bowl, erase it with water, and have her drink it on an empty stomach. She shall be healed by the will of God Almighty.

**(Chapter Eleven)** To stop a hemorrhage, if you desire this, write the seven seals on seven pieces of paper and recite the invocation over them seven times. Eat them <note>The text suggests consuming the papers or the water used to wash the ink from them</note> one after another, and it shall be cured by the will of God Almighty.

**(Chapter Twelve)** To facilitate childbirth, if you desire this, write the first, second, and third seals on a piece of paper and hang it on her left side. With the rest, create a <term>protective circle: original "tahut," a talismanic boundary</term> around her after reciting the invocation once. She shall give birth immediately by the will of God Almighty.

**(Chapter Thirteen)** For the flow of milk in the breasts: If you desire this, write the seven seals <term>al-khawatim al-sab'a: a set of seven specific talismanic symbols believed to represent the Great Name of God</term> on a piece of paper <note>original: "kaghad," a Persian-derived word for paper</note> and hang them upon her breasts after reciting the invocation once. She shall see wonders from the abundance of milk.

**(Chapter Fourteen)** To remove fever: If you desire this, write the seven seals on a piece of paper and hang it upon the person. Write it a second time and fumigate them with the paper, one by one, in the person's presence after reciting the invocation three times; for it shall bring healing.

**(Chapter Fifteen)** To burn the Jinn: If you desire this, write the seven seals on a rag and twist it into a wick. Light it and bring it close to the nose of the afflicted person <note>original: "al-ma'rud," referring to someone possessed or touched by a spirit</note> while you recite the invocation without a fixed number of repetitions. The Jinn inhabiting the body will be burned in that very moment and hour.

**(Chapter Sixteen)** To remove fear and panic: If you desire this, write the seventh seal on the palm of the hand and recite the invocation three times; the fear will vanish immediately.

**(Chapter Seventeen)** To face enemies: If you desire this, take a red silk thread and recite the invocation over it once, then tie a knot. Continue this until you have completed seven knots for the seven seals. Each seal is written and then a knot is tied over it. Hang it around your neck and face your enemies, even if they number in the thousands; for God will grant you victory over them.

**(Chapter Eighteen)** For entering before rulers: If you desire this, write the seven seals on a piece of paper and recite the invocation over them three times. Place the paper in your turban <note>original: "imama"</note> and enter before the ruler <note>original: "al-hakim," meaning a judge, governor, or person of authority</note>; he will hold you in great awe, such as he has never felt for another.

**(Chapter Nineteen)** To release a "bound" man or a "tied" groom <note>original: "al-marbut," a common folk belief where a man is magically rendered impotent or unable to consummate a marriage</note>: If you desire this, write the first seal on his right hand, the second on his left hand, the third on his right thigh, the fourth on his left thigh, the fifth on his back, the sixth on his chest, and the seventh on his navel. The incense should be burning while the incantation <term>al-’azimah: a formal magical command or spell</term> is recited. Also, write on his right palm:

> "And all faces shall be humbled before the Ever-Living, the Sustainer..." (until) "...the one who carries a burden of wrongdoing." <note>Quran, Surah Taha 20:111</note>

—And on his left palm—

> "But whoever performs righteous deeds..." (until) "...nor any loss." <note>Quran, Surah Taha 20:112</note>

The man should hold his hands over the incense while you recite the incantation seven times; then he shall be released by the permission of God Almighty.

**(Chapter Twenty)** To make a palm branch walk on the road to find lost items: If you desire this, write the seven seals on a green palm branch three spans <note>a "shibr" is the distance between the tip of the thumb and the tip of the little finger</note> in length. Write the fourth seal on a piece of paper, split the end of the branch, and place the paper in its "mouth." Recite the invocation until the branch moves toward the suspected location and stops upon it.

**(Chapter Twenty-One)** To identify a thief: If you desire this, take a wooden cup used for measuring grain. Write the names of the suspects upon the cup. Tie the head of a nail to the fingertips of the suspects and recite the invocation without a fixed number. Say: "If the owner of this name took anything and is the thief, then turn the cup and throw it to the ground. If he is not the thief, let the cup not turn." Do this for all the suspects, one by one, until the cup turns at the name of one of them.

**(Chapter Twenty-Two)** To extract buried treasure: If you desire this, take a bowl of red copper and engrave the invocation and the seals around its circumference. Recite the invocation over it; it will move to the location of the buried hoard, whether it contains money or otherwise.

**(Chapter Twenty-Three)** To remove sorcery from one who is bewitched: If you desire this, write the third seal on a barber's razor. Recite the invocation over it once and shave the head of the bewitched person with it; whatever afflicts them will vanish instantly.

**(Chapter Twenty-Four)** To reveal theft: If you desire this, take a red glass and engrave the first seal upon it. Recite the invocation over it three times and place it under your head when you sleep; you shall see the one who took your property and know their location.

**(Chapter Twenty-Five)** To send the "voices" <term>Hatif: a spiritual voice or unseen messenger used to communicate or strike fear into a target</term>: If you desire this, take a piece of hide from a red horse and engrave the seven seals upon it. Recite the invocation over it seven times. Take the tongue of a sparrow and place it in the center of the inscribed hide, then put it in a dark place. State your need and do not sleep that night, but recite the invocation without a fixed number of repetitions. The "servant" <term>khadim: a spiritual entity assigned to perform a specific task for the practitioner</term> will appear to the targeted person and quickly fulfill your need.

**(Chapter Twenty-Six)** To confuse the enemy: If you desire this, take the skin of a mouse and write the seven seals upon it. **(Chapter Twenty-Seven)** For riding horses: If you desire this, write the seven seals on a piece of paper and recite the invocation over it three times. Hang it upon yourself, and you shall see wonders.

**(Chapter Twenty-Eight)** For carrying weapons: If you desire this, write the seventh seal on a leaf from a sycamore tree <note>original: "jamiz," the sycamore-fig, often associated with ancient protective magic</note> and recite the invocation over it three times. Hang it upon yourself and strike whomever you wish with a weapon; no one shall ever defeat you, by the grace of God.

**(Chapter Twenty-Nine)** To attract customers: If you desire this, write the seven seals for him on green paper and recite the invocation over it once. Hang it in the place of business, and you shall see wonders from the abundance of customers.

**(Chapter Thirty)** To remove the "look" <note>original: "nadhra," a specific type of spiritual gaze</note> and the Evil Eye from Jinn and Men: If you desire this, write the seven seals on the collar-lining <note>original: "tawliqa," the fabric lining or facing of a garment's neck</note> of a white shirt. Wrap it with red silk thread, fumigate it with incense, and recite the invocation over it eleven times; the person shall be healed, God willing.

**(Chapter Thirty-One)** To restore the throat <note>original: "radd al-huluq," likely referring to curing throat ailments or swellings</note>: If you desire this, write the first seal on the palm of the person who has the illness and recite the invocation over it without a fixed number of repetitions. After that, hang the writing upon them and they shall be healed, God the Exalted willing.

- **(Chapter Thirty-Two)** To make the house-spirit speak: If you desire this, recite the invocation without a fixed number of repetitions and command the servant to bring the spirit of the house <term>'Amir: the spiritual entity believed to reside in and "populate" a building</term>; for you shall see wonders.

- **(Chapter Thirty-Three)** To dismiss the house-spirits from any place: If you desire this, recite the invocation once and command them to depart. They shall depart from you, and not one of them will remain.

- **(Chapter Thirty-Four)** To employ spiritual helpers and ensure their obedience to you: If you desire this, sit in a place empty of people. Keep your clothing and body pure <note>referring to ritual purity or "tahara"</note> for a full night and recite the invocation without a fixed number of repetitions. The servant shall come to you and inform you of whatever you wish.

**(Chapter Thirty-Five)** To remove bedbugs from a place: If you desire this, write the first four seals on four pieces of paper and bury each paper in one of the corners of the place without reciting the invocation; they shall depart from it.

**(Chapter Thirty-Six)** To remove mice: If you desire this, take a raw clay shard <note>original: "shaqfa niyya," a piece of unbaked or sun-dried pottery</note> and write the seven seals upon it. Recite the invocation over it three times and bury it under the threshold of the place or in the field; they will depart from it as long as the shard remains buried.
