### ### (Chapter Thirty-Seven)
**For the removal of ants** — If you desire this, take the skin of a goat and write the seven seals upon it. Recite the invocation once and wash the writing with water. Sprinkle that water in the place where the ants are, and they shall depart from it.

### ### (Chapter Thirty-Eight)
**For gout** <note>Original: *al-niqrah*. Historically, this term refers to gout or sharp, localized joint pain.</note> — If you desire this, write the fifth seal upon a piece of hide and write the name of the King of that day. Read the invocation over it and place the hide upon the site of the pain; it shall depart, by the leave of God Almighty.

### ### (Chapter Thirty-Nine)
**For the removal of *al-maraq*** <note>Original: *al-maraq*. In this context, it likely refers to a skin effusion, excessive fluid, or a wasting of the flesh.</note> **from a person** — If you desire this, write the first seal for them on a piece of white silk while incense is burning. Recite the invocation over it three times and strike the site of the ailment with the silk; it shall depart, by the leave of God Almighty.

### ### (Chapter Forty)
**To remove warts that appear on the body** — If you desire this, write the seventh seal in the palm of your hand and recite the invocation over it three times. Strike the site of the wart with your palm; it shall depart, by the leave of God Almighty.

### ### (Chapter Forty-One)
**To sicken enemies** — Take a red catfish and write the first seal on a morsel of bread. Place it in the fish's mouth and recite the invocation over it eleven times. When the catfish dies, the "arrow" <note>A metaphorical reference to the magical strike or curse.</note> will take effect. Fear God! <note>The phrase "Fear God" (*Ittaqu-llah*) serves as a recurrent warning against using these harmful spells without a just cause.</note>

### ### (Chapter Forty-Two)
**For the illness of enemies** — Take an egg from a black hen and write the second seal upon it. Recite the invocation over it forty-one times. The person targeted shall not cease being ill until the egg is washed and the writing upon it is erased; then they shall recover.

### ### (Chapter Forty-Three)
**To cause an enemy to bleed** — If you desire this, take red paper <note>Original: *kaghad*, a term for paper of Persian/Chinese origin.</note> and write the seven seals upon it. Place it inside a Persian reed and seal it with Alexandrian wax. Tie a red silk thread to the end of the reed segment and recite the invocation over it three times. Bury it under a water channel that flows toward the East. The targeted person will suffer from bleeding as long as the reed remains buried in the channel.

### ### (Chapter Forty-Four)
**To inflict scabies** <term>al-jarab: a contagious, itchy skin condition caused by mites</term> **upon enemies** — If you desire this, take white wax and fashion a figure from it. Take some lime and place it inside the wax figure. Take a ceramic jar and place these items inside after writing the seventh seal. Place the jar in the ashes of a furnace. The targeted person will be unable to eat or drink due to the intense itching of their skin. Read the invocation ten times before burying it in the ashes. Fear God!

### ### (Chapter Forty-Five)
**To inflict lice upon an enemy** — If you desire this, take a dried piece of dough, some fine oil, and bean flour, and fashion them into a figure. Take a garment for the figure and write the sixth seal upon it—the garment must be of black wool. Take the water used to wash that garment and mix it with honey. Take some ants, lice, and fleas and cast them into that gourd <note>The text likely refers to the ceramic jar or a hollowed gourd used as a vessel.</note>. Recite the invocation over them eleven times and bury it in the location where the target resides. You shall see wonders. Fear God!

### ### (Chapter Forty-Six)
**To inflict fever upon an oppressor** — If you desire this, take a piece of glass and write the seven seals upon it. Recite the invocation over it an indefinite number of times and say: "O servants of these names, cast fever upon the owner of this name." Their body shall burn with fever and they shall not recover until the writing is wiped away. Fear God!

### ### (Chapter Forty-Seven)
**To inflict a "striker"** <note>Original: *al-darib*. Likely refers to a spirit or force that causes physical pain or blows.</note> **upon an enemy** — If you desire this, take a "trace" <term>athar: a personal item belonging to the target, such as a piece of clothing or hair, used to establish a magical link</term> of the desired person...

**For the splitting of the eye of one of your enemies** — If you desire this, take an apple and recite the invocation over it three times. Place it where the wind and air blow, and write upon it the name of the intended person and the name of his mother; you shall see a wonder.

### ### (Chapter Fifty)
**To set the Jinn** <term>Jinn: Supernatural spirits in Islamic cosmology who can be commanded by a sorcerer to harass a victim.</term> **upon one of your enemies** — If you desire this, take the collar of a blue shirt and write the seven seals upon it. Recite the invocation sixteen times and bury it in a ruined place; he shall be afflicted quickly.

### ### (Chapter Fifty-One)
**For the stoning of the house of one of your enemies** <note>Historically, "stoning" (*rajm*) in a magical context refers to poltergeist-like activity where stones are seemingly thrown by unseen hands.</note> — If you desire this, take a raw pottery shard and write the second seal upon it. Recite the invocation over it ten times and bury it under the threshold of the intended house; you shall see a wonder as long as the shard remains buried under the threshold of that place.

Sprinkle them in the place where you desire this to happen, and it shall immediately become inhabited by Jinn.

### ### (Chapter Fifty-Three)
**For the separation of enemies** — Write the second seal for them on a black rag and recite the invocation over it seven times. Bury it in the place where the work is intended, and they shall be separated from that moment.

### ### (Chapter Fifty-Four)

Erase the writing with water from a bathhouse and sprinkle it in the house you desire, and they shall depart.

### ### (Chapter Fifty-Five)
**To break the millstone of an oppressor** — If you desire this, take some flour and recite the invocation over it fifteen times. Throw the flour into the heart of the millstone, and it shall break at that very hour and moment.

### ### (Chapter Fifty-Six)
**To disable the oven of an oppressor or break its stone** — If you desire this, take a piece of paper and write the second seal upon it. Place it inside a loaf of dough and throw it into the oven; the bread shall not bake and the stone shall break.

<margin>H</margin> ### ### (Chapter Fifty-Seven)
**For the harm of enemies** — If you desire this, take some bran and recite the invocation over it ten times. Sprinkle it in the place intended for the work, and you shall see a wonder.

### ### (Chapter Sixty) 
**To sink the waters of a well, a waterwheel, or a spring**

If you desire this, take a lead tablet and engrave the seventh seal upon it. Recite the invocation over it ten times while the incense is burning, then throw it into the well or the waterwheel; its water will sink and dry up immediately.

### ### (Chapter Sixty-One) 
**For opening hidden treasures** <note>Original: "al-matalib." This refers to locations where treasure is believed to be buried or hidden by magical means.</note>

If you desire this, recite the invocation nineteen times over the occupied site; it will open so you may take your provision from it—and be among the thankful—even if there is a barrier or a <term>guardian spirit</term> <note>Original: "rasad." A supernatural sentinel, usually a Jinn, tasked with protecting hidden treasure.</note>. If so, write the first four seals on four pieces of paper and place a paper in each corner. Write the remaining three seals and place them in the center of the site. Recite the invocation a second time, seven times in total, and all those barriers shall vanish. This is a complete ritual application <note>Original: "tasrif." The practical use or manifestation of a magical formula.</note>. 

However, it requires a great spiritual retreat <term>Khulwa</term> <note>A period of secluded prayer and fasting used by practitioners to gain spiritual power or command over spirits.</note> of seven days. He who attempts these matters without the retreat will not achieve them; the retreat must come first. Through it, one obtains a slipper <note>Original: "babuj." A traditional leather slipper.</note> with two sides: if you wear the first side, you may walk upon water; if you wear the smaller side, you may walk through doors. It also grants a cap <note>Original: "taqiya." A skullcap.</note> with two sides: the first side makes you invisible to the eyes of people, and the second side makes your face shine with light like the moon. It also grants a purse that never empties of money and a King who is entrusted to serve you night and day. It has many ritual applications, but the foundation is the fear of God and the service of the invocation.

->( This is the description of the Retreat )<-

Know—may God guide us and you to goodness, O seeker—that you must take for yourself a place secluded from people. Be pure of garment, hand, and location. Burn sweet-smelling incense, then dismiss the <term>Ammar</term> <note>Spirits believed to inhabit houses and buildings. They must be "dismissed" with a specific spell so they do not interfere with the ritual.</note>. Fast for the sake of God the Exalted for seven days, and break your fast only on that which does not contain a soul and did not come from a soul <note>A common occult practice of avoiding meat, dairy, and eggs to maintain spiritual purity.</note>. Recite the invocation seven times after every obligatory prayer while the incense is burning and the door is locked against you. Sit in your retreat throughout your night, and beware of sleep, for it will cause your work to fail.

On the first night, something like snakes will come to you; do not let that terrify you, for they are merely illusions. On the second night, something in the form of terrifying wild beasts will come to you; do not let that terrify you. On the third night, the <term>Ammar</term> <note>House-spirits.</note> will come to you. On the fourth night, <term>Marada</term> <note>The most powerful and rebellious class of Jinn.</note> will come and try to frighten you; pay no mind to that. On the fifth night, various types of Jinn will come to you; do not let that terrify you. On the sixth night, the <term>Khuddam</term> <note>Servant spirits of the invocation.</note> will come to you. They will say, "What is this? Tell us what you seek." Do not speak to them, for if you speak to them, you will perish.

On the seventh night, at the end of the night, a group will come to you in the form of sweepers and carpet-spreaders. They will furnish the place. Then Kings, soldiers, armies, and tribes will come to you in the manner of Sultans. The group will bring chairs and set them up to the right and left. Then they will bring a great, magnificent throne and set it in the middle of those chairs. A King will sit upon that throne; he is the greatest, strongest, and most massive of them in stature. Drums will be beaten and various entertainments will be set up. Do not be distracted by this, but recite the invocation while remaining alert <note>Original: "ala hassik." Remaining focused and aware of one's senses.</note>, for if you become distracted by them, you will perish. 

At that moment, the King will say to you, "What is it you seek?" Say to him, "I seek such and such, and give me the cap and the items I have enumerated for you." He will give you those things. I charge you, O you who have reached this invocation: do not hand it over to anyone except those who deserve it. You must fear God, and I am innocent of your soul if you perform evil deeds.

->( If you wish that the assistants do not harm you )<-

During the retreat, take the seven seals and write them on a piece of paper and carry it upon your head. Encircle yourself with the invocation as a protective circle <note>Original: "tahwit." The practice of drawing a magical circle for protection.</note>, and they shall never harm you, by the permission of God.

# ( This is the Description of the Incense for All Works )

Liquid storax, sandarac, <term>male frankincense</term> <note>Original: "luban dhakir." This refers to high-quality, pure resin tears of Boswellia sacra, preferred for spiritual rites.</note>, mace, wild rue, coriander, and <term>canary seed</term> <note>Original: "lisan 'asfur." Literally "sparrow's tongue," a common name in herbology for various seeds or the ash tree seed.</note>. Grind all of these together, knead them with rose water, form them into small pellets, and preserve them for when they are needed.

## ( This is the Blessed Invocation )

In the name of God, the Clement, the Merciful. In the name of God, the Sovereign, the Possessor of the kingdom and the celestial realm, of might, power, ability, and absolute dominion; the Master of the celestial and earthly realms; the Creator of the Throne, the Footstool, the heavens, and the earth. Blessed be God, Lord of the Worlds, the One of reaching power and towering might, the Light of Lights, the Spirit of Spirits, the All-Glorious, the All-Holy, Lord of the Angels and the Spirit. Glory be to Him, the Most High, who is transcendent in His nearness and near in His transcendence; the One who reveals Himself through His dominion, unique in might and majesty; the Knower whose knowledge encompasses the end and the beginning. There is no god but He, the Unique, the Everlasting, the Eternal Authority, before whom kings have humbled themselves, and every monarch has become a subject to His greatness. He is the Originator of the heavens and the earth, who makes the angels messengers with wings—two, three, and four; He increases in creation what He wills, for God has power over all things.

I adjure you, O pure, hidden, and virtuous spiritual beings, and your persons of sublime essence, and your bright, radiant, and beautiful lights—you who are entrusted with the celestial spirits, the lunar stations, and the hours of time—by the One who revealed Himself to the mountain and turned it to dust out of awe, so that Moses fell down unconscious from His majesty. When he recovered, he said: "Glory be to Thee! I turn to Thee in repentance, and I am the first of the believers." The Throne dripped with sweat from His awe, and kings were humbled by His greatness and vanished; necks bowed to the majesty of the splendor of His greatness and multiplied. A mighty power of His authority called them, and they answered with humility and servitude, then they were scattered, and He revived them after their death and they gathered. Kings were humbled before Him and retreated; they answered Him with humility and acknowledgement of His Lordship, and they multiplied. That is God, your Lord—so where are you being turned? Indeed, your Lord is God, who created the heavens and the earth in six days, then established Himself upon the Throne; He covers the night with the day, seeking it rapidly, and the sun, the moon, and the stars are subjected by His command. Unquestionably, His is the creation and the command; blessed be God, Lord of the Worlds.

Come to me, O assembly of spiritual beings, with your beautiful lights, your shining rays, your pleasing characters, and your pure breaths. For I have adjured you by the Swift, the Exalted, the Hidden, the Known, the Sought—which is the <term>Greatest Name of God</term>—and it is this: **Bajmash** <note>A magical word of power often found in medieval Arabic grimoires, used to compel spirits.</note>. Appear, O Unique One, O Compeller, O Most Thankful, O Firm One, O Supporter, O All-Aware, O Pure One, O our God and the God of all things; there is no god but Thee, O Possessor of Majesty and Honor.

O God, I ask Thee to subject Thy angels to me at this time of mine; indeed, Thou hast power over all things. <margin>3</margin> Answer, O **Ruqya’il**! <margin>2</margin> Answer, O **Samsama’il**! <margin>M</margin> Answer, O **Mika’il**! Answer, O **Sarfiya’il**! Answer, O **Aniya’il**! Answer, O **Kasfiya’il**! Answer, O **Mudhib**, and you, O **Murra**, and you, O **Ahmar**, and you, O **Burqan**, and you, O **Shamhurush**, and you, O **Zawba’a**, and you, O **Maymun**! Answer, O **Israfil**, and you, O **Azra’il**, and you, O **Samkiya’il**, and you, O **Nuriya’il**, and you, O **Metatron**, and you, O **Shamhun**, and you, O **Halbakh**.

Answer and be present, and bring forth your men, your elders, your youths, your weaned ones, your infants, your great and your small, your servants, your males, your females, your those who walk and those who ride, those present and those absent, and your helpers in the land, the sea, and the air. Be a help to me in fulfilling my need.

Answer, O King **Ruqya’il**, the one entrusted with the sphere of the Sun, by the might of the Mighty One who is fortified in the might of His might, the Unique, the Everlasting, the Eternal Authority. Answer, O King **Gabriel** <note>Original: "Jibra'il."</note>, the one entrusted with the Moon, by the dominion of the Majestic One who reveals Himself in the praise of the light of His greatness, unique in oneness and governance, the Appreciative, the Most Thankful. I adjure you, O King **Samsama’il**, the one entrusted with the sphere of Mars, by the greatness of the Great One who is supreme in the glory of His divinity, the Eternal Authority, the Unique, the Everlasting. **There is no god but He**, the Mighty, the Compeller. I adjure you, O King **Mika’il**, the one entrusted with the sphere of Mercury, by the strength of the Eternal Power, the Compassionate, the All-Bounteous...

The Giver of Honor, the Giver of Dishonor, the Possessor of towering strength and complete, sufficient power; there is no god but He. He honors whom He wills and humbles whom He wills, the Generous, the Most High. I adjure you, O <term>Sarfiyail</term> <note>Original: "Sarfiyā’īl." In this system of planetary magic, this angel is appointed over the sphere of Jupiter (al-Mushtarī).</note>, the King entrusted with the sphere of Jupiter, by the might of the Mighty One to whom all kings are humbled, and before whose greatness, divinity, and majesty all are but servants—complying with His subdual, desiring His favor, and acting as His subjects. He is the Towering One, the Unique, and the Wondrous Light. 

I adjure you, O <term>Anayail</term> <note>Original: "‘Anāyā’īl." Commonly identified as Anael, the angel entrusted with the sphere of Venus (al-Zuhara).</note>, the King entrusted with the sphere of Venus, by the eternity of the Eternal Ancient in His timelessness; the Great in His pride; the Creator, the All-Knowing; the Creator of every soul, its Originator and its Subduer—and He is capable of all things. 

I adjure you, O <term>Kasfiyail</term> <note>Original: "Kasfiyā’īl." Commonly identified as Cassiel, the angel entrusted with the sphere of Saturn (Zuhal).</note>, the King entrusted with the sphere of Saturn, by the majesty of the Illustrious, the Most Grand, the Most High in the praise of His divinity. That is God, your Lord; there is no god but He. Everything is perishing except His Face; to Him belongs the judgment and the governance, and He is capable of all things. 

[You must] answer my call, be guided by my word, and obey my conjuration with safety, security, goodness, and benevolence in the subjugation of the earthly kings, so that they may answer

Answer, O <term>Madhhab</term> <note>The Jinn king of Sunday, associated with the Sun.</note>, and you, O <term>Murrah</term> <note>The Jinn king of Monday, associated with the Moon.</note>, and you, O <term>Burqan</term> <note>The Jinn king of Wednesday, associated with Mercury.</note>, and you, O <term>Shamhurush</term> <note>The Jinn king of Thursday, associated with Jupiter.</note>, and you, O <term>Abyad</term> <note>The Jinn king of Friday, associated with Venus.</note>, and you, O <term>Maymun</term> <note>The Jinn king of Saturday, associated with Saturn.</note>. For I call upon you by <term>Syriac names</term> <note>Original: "Asmā’ Suryāniyya." In Islamic occultism, these are "primordial" names of power, often derived from Hebrew or Aramaic, believed to be the language of the angels and ancient prophets.</note>, divine conjurations, clear signs, inscribed talismans, and exhortations. I call you by the perfect, reaching signs of God. I have summoned you by God, by the greatness of God, by the light of God, and by the magnificent majesty of God. I have drawn you from the easts of the earth and its wests by the Greatest Spirit and the honored Prophet, **Solomon son of David** (peace be upon him), and by the Essence of God which no other essence resembles—the One who made you kings, supported you with victory, and made you deputies so that you may act within His kingdom as He wills, where no preoccupation distracts you from His worship and no neglect keeps you from His remembrance. 

Therefore, wherever [the work] is thus and so, come and attend to the fulfillment of my need, cooperating and hastening to meet it. Open the doors and lift the veil. Welcome, welcome to the beloved ones, the possessors of merit, light, coolness, warmth, and might. May the peace of God the Exalted, His mercy, His blessings, and His purest greetings be upon you. By this, may your honored presence and your pure spirits attend. May God increase you in honor, might, and the obedience you hold. May God make your efforts appreciated, your sins forgiven, and your seeker supported and victorious. 

O gathering of spiritual souls, be an aid in the fulfillment of my need, cooperating in whatever matter I ask of you (and it is thus and so). For if you disobey, no heaven shall shade you, no earth shall carry you, and no mountains shall shelter you. The sky shall rain fire and thunderbolts upon you, the valleys shall swallow you, and the mountains shall tremble because of you. There shall be no obedience [for you from others] and no escape for you. So obey and be an aid in the fulfillment of my need, gathering for it by the right of these names: *Shah Hashah Ash Lash Shamkhitha Yakhuth Bashmakh Shamakh* <note>These are phonetic transcriptions of names of power; "Bashmakh Shamakh" is often interpreted as "The Lofty One" or "Lord of the Sublime."</note>, the Exalted over every blessing. 

O <term>Metatron</term> <note>Original: "Mīṭaṭrūn." The supreme archangel and mediator between the divine and the earthly realms.</note>, hear my call and understand the fulfillment of my need. *Ah Ah Mah Mah*—<term>Sabaoth</term> <note>Original: "Asba'ut." From the Hebrew 'Sabaoth,' meaning 'Hosts,' as in 'Lord of Hosts.'</note>, a King who cannot be contested, a Giant who cannot be described, and an Eternal Refuge who does not consume food. "For every matter there is a settled time" <note>A reference to Quran 6:67.</note>. *Mantiqat Ashkush Matrush H (2) Mah (2) La Kaf Laghaf*. 

"It was but a single blast, and behold, they were all brought before Us" <note>Quran 36:53, frequently used in magic to compel spirits to appear instantly.</note>. "And proclaim to the people the Pilgrimage; they will come to you on foot and on every lean camel; they will come from every distant pass" <note>Quran 22:27, used here as a metaphor for the spirits arriving from all directions.</note>. 

Hasten to respond: Al-Waha! Al-'Ajal! Al-Sa'ah! <note>Traditional closing formula: "Hasten! Hurry! This very hour!"</note>

*Kak'ush Laharush Yakush Afriyush Layk Matrush Larish Kaykuwash*. Hasten, answering and submissive to God, the Lord of the Worlds. May God bless you and be upon you.

<meta>This page transitions from the specific planetary talismans of the previous section toward the general liturgical closing of these rituals. ### (And this is the dismissal of the invocation, as you say)

O you who have believed, when the call is made for prayer on the day of Jumu'ah <note>Friday, the communal day of prayer in Islam.</note>, then hasten to the remembrance of God and leave business. That is better for you, if you only knew. And when the prayer has been concluded, disperse within the land and seek from the bounty of God, and remember God often that you may succeed. And when they saw a transaction or a diversion, they rushed to it and left you standing. Say, "What is with God is better than diversion and than a transaction, and God is the best of providers." <note>This is a translation of the Quran, Surah al-Jumu'ah (62:9-11). In this context, it is used ritualistically to "dismiss" the spiritual forces and return to the physical world.</note> The invocation is completed with all its variations, and praise be to God.

**Note:** I have observed in many books that the "Invocation of the Great and Middle Importance" <note>The "Aham-iya" (أهمية) invocations are a core part of medieval Arabic occult science, based on a specific seven-letter acrostic.</note> contains many errors, so I wished to correct it for you in its true form. I speak with the success granted by God.

## (The Invocation of the Great Importance)

->In the name of God, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful<-

O God, I ask You by Your name: <term>Aham Saqak Hala’ Yas</term> <note>أهم سقك حلع يص: A famous seven-word magical acrostic used in the science of letters (Jafr). Each letter corresponds to a divine attribute and a planet.</note> O God, by the right of Your name, the Great, the Most Magnificent <term>Aham</term>, the First by Your remembrance, the Self-Existent. O God, I ask You by the right of Your name *Aham*, by Your divinity, O God, O One, O Unique, O Solitary, O Eternal, You are the God of those in the heavens and those on the earth. You are the Creator and You are the Judge over them. O Guide, O Granter of Security, O Overseer, O Firm One. *Aham Saqak Hala’ Yas*. God is a Noble King, All-Hearing, Capable, Clement, Subtle, All-Knowing, Certainty, Truthful.

O God, I ask You by the right of Your Great, Most Magnificent name, through whose radiant flashes the heavens are filled, and by whose light the dawn was split, so that the luminous celestial bodies <note>Original: "ajrām nayyirāt."</note> drew strength from its rays. By Your resolve, You caused the <term>eloquent spheres</term> <note>The "Speaking" or "Eloquent" Spheres (Nawāṭiq al-Aflāk) refers to the belief that the planetary spheres emit sounds or "voices" as they move.</note> to turn, and by it, You guided those who seek Your provision to reach their hopes in the courtyards of Your judgment. By it, You opened the doors of response and petition for Your servants, and by it, You inspired Your saints, so their prayer became answered through it—and You are the Cause. You built the abodes of uprightness for the gnostics, and by it, You opened the closed veil of the heavy clouds of generosity and honor. 

My God, it is Your name by which You surpassed the <term>closing of the heavens</term> <note>Reference to "ratq," the primordial state before creation was separated.</note> and through which You manifested the meaning of Your name "The Truth." By it, You revealed the hidden essences from Your protected sanctuary. By it, You formed the created beings through the <term>Kaf and the Nun</term> <note>Referring to the divine command "Be" (Kun), spelled with the Arabic letters Kaf and Nun.</note>. You swore by its divinity over the physical forms, and You clothed the human reality with the letters of its receptivity. Thus, the angels of acceptance prostrated to it in obedience to Your command, and in veneration of the breath of Your secret that flows through the forms by the image of Your management and Your design.

(O God, enrich me through You, rather than by any of Your creation) 
<note>This phrase is to be repeated 7 times.</note>

For there is none to repel Your command and none to override Your judgment. Let the eye of my heart be opened so that the suns of illumination appear from it, and let the density of the clouds of my veils be uncovered so that I may perceive the beauty of character. O God, clothe me in the garment of contentment from that which You have bestowed. Let my submission prevail over my own free will, so that I may bear witness that You are You. O God, purify me through the manifestation of faith from the filth of the lapses of pleasure. O God, protect me with Your protection from falling into errors, so that I may become, by Your pure, absolute generosity, connected and not severed. For there is no giver but You, no preventer of Your gift, and no repeller of Your decree. 

(O God) O Most Gracious, O Most Merciful, O Ever-Living, O Self-Subsisting, O All-Knowing, O Magnificent, O Possessor of Majesty and Honor. <term>K.H.Y.A.S. H.M.A.S.Q.</term> <note>These are the "mysterious letters" or "disjointed letters" that open various chapters of the Quran, used here for their perceived talismanic power.</note>

(1) Like the water which We send down from the sky, and the vegetation of the earth mingles with it, but then it becomes dry stubble which the winds scatter. (2) He is God, other than whom there is no deity, Knower of the unseen and the witnessed. He is the Entirely Merciful, the Especially Merciful. (3) The Day of the <term>Approaching Hour</term> <note>Al-Āzifah: A name for the Day of Judgment.</note>, when hearts are at the throats, choking [with grief]. For the wrongdoers there will be no devoted friend and no intercessor who is obeyed. (4) A soul shall know what it has brought [with it]. So I swear by the retreating stars—those that run [their courses] and disappear—and by the night as it closes in, and by the dawn as it breathes. (5) Sad. By the Quran full of admonition. But those who disbelieve are in pride and dissension. 

O God, I ask You by the right of Your Great, Most Magnificent name: *Aham Saqak Hala'...*

# Chapter on the Great Milanosy Oath

There have been many recurring accounts regarding the oath called "The Great Milanosy." I have searched for it extensively and found many different versions containing numerous errors. After great effort, I have found this oath in its correct form. I present it to my fellow scholars with a generous heart to save them the trouble of searching for it. I say, and with God lies all success:

->( The Great Milanosy Oath )<-

In the name of God, the Beginner without a beginning, and the Ender without an end; to Him belongs praise and prostration forever.
*Anih Ti Mashush Kalmish Jalha-ya Neil* <note>Original: "آنه تي ما شوش كلميش جلهـ ـ ـ يا نيل" - These are esoteric names of God often found in the "Barhatiyya" or similar conjurations.</note>

"When the children of Israel went out of Egypt, and the house of Jacob from a barbarian people." <note>This is a direct translation of the Arabic which itself translates Psalm 114:1. In this context, it is used as a "word of power."</note>
*Bar Baryanthila'ish Aal Yah Bamajtuhoula'ish Aklash Abrik-hash Moush Kalmoush.* **Israfel** <note>The Angel of the Trumpet</note> blew the trumpet of grandeur, and the mountains were leveled, and a light from the light of Majesty shone upon them. *Abjaha Bouri Aal Zuyal*, the cleaver of the peaks of mountains. I ask you, O master *Tahay-tagh-nilyal* the Luminous, by the right of these names: *Kalsh* (2), *Ghalsh* (2), *Tawtaytash* (2), *Dahalshakh* (2), *Amar Yoush* (2), *Ayar Yash* (2). To God, the inhabitants of the earth and the heavens prostrate by Your light, *Kalmoush Ashmakhtall Takikh* the Great. How great are the names of God! Prostrate to God by the right of these Divinity-linked names and the profound Solomonic conjurations. All those in the regions of the heavens and the earth prostrated. O *Lamajab*, the glorification of Adam and the *Al-Han*...

<summary>This page presents a complex magical square (Wafq) surrounded by the four archangels and introduces the "Great Milanosy Oath," a Solomonic conjuration used for spiritual authority.</summary>
<keywords>Milanosy Oath, Magic Square, Talisman, Gabriel, Michael, Israfel, Azrael, Solomonic Magic, Psalm 114, Occult formulas</keywords>

<meta>This page concludes the "Great Milanosy Oath" and begins a new section regarding a "Proven Dispatch" (irsal), a ritual intended to send a spiritual messenger to a specific person. ...David! O solace of the bliss of the righteous fathers dwelling in the **bosom of Abraham**. O, the misery of those who defy these names and do not obey their glory and might! I have drawn the servants of this, my oath, by the signs of the magnificent Moses, the possessor of the **two horns of radiant light** <note>This refers to "Dhu al-Qarnayn," a figure often identified with Alexander the Great or a saintly king, but here linked to Moses, perhaps referencing the "rays of light" or "horns" described in Exodus after he descended from Sinai.</note>. *Alshaqum, Ahmattum, Tum, Aklash, Abratkannash, Bazmanash, Heyshurash, Ahmalush, Amatrash* <note>These are "Barhatiyya" names—ancient, rhythmic power words common in Middle Eastern magic, believed to be in a primordial or "Syriac" language.</note>.

I adjure you by the Great, Most Grand Name, by the Most Noble King, and by the light of the face of God: Answer, O Master **Tahitamghilyal** (and take charge of such-and-such) by the right of *Aheel, Eel, Ah, Bisharlish, Tayshafkak, Kafalush, Hanshakush, Taltayeel*, the Greatest God. Answer, O Master **Tahitamghilyal** (and do such-and-such) by the right of *Hinjayl, Kaytalifish, Kalkalum, Maykalkalum, Mashkalum, ‘Asfhash, Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh, Adonai, Tzabaoth, El Shaddai* <note>original: "أهيا شراهيا أدوناى أصباووت ال شداى" – These are famous Hebrew names for God: "I am that I am," "Lord of Hosts," and "God Almighty."</note>.

Answer, O Master **Tahitamghilyal** (and do what I have commanded you) by the right of *Hawn, Mahwn, Mimahwn, Hayhwn, Hanayhwn, Manaykahwn, Dahihwn, Ahmahayhwn, Hamnayhalmwn, Sha’rahayhwn, Talhatayahayhwn, Kamahwn, Heej-hwn, Kashmeel-hwn*, God! Perform for me what I have requested! Haste, haste! <note>original: "الوحا" (al-waha)</note> Speed, speed! <note>original: "العجل" (al-'ajal)</note> This very hour! <note>original: "الساعة" (al-sa'ah)</note> It is finished.

->### ( A Proven Dispatch )<-

You shall draw the following **Magic Square** <term>Muthallath: Literally "triangle," but in this context, it refers to a 3x3 magic square or a specific geometric talisman</term>, and this is its form as you see. Then, you shall draw an identical seal on its back, writing the letter *Alif* (ا) on the back of the *Alif*, the *Jeem* (ج) on the back of the *Jeem*, the *Zay* (ز) on the back of the *Zay*, and the *Tah* (ط) on the back of the *Tah*. In the center, write the name of the desired person, then write the "authorization" <note>The specific command or task</note> around it, saying:

| | **ط** (Tah) | |
| :---: | :---: | :---: |
| **ز** (Zay) | **هـ** (Ha) | **ج** (Jeem) |
| | **ا** (Alif) | |

<note>The letters T-H-Z-J-A represent the Abjad numbers 9, 5, 7, 3, 1. When combined, they form the name "Ajhazat," a spiritual entity often invoked in these rituals.</note>

"Compel for me, O **Ajhazat**, (Name, son of Name) in such-and-such a place, in my form and likeness (or authorize him with whatever you desire)."

This square is to be written in this order on seven slips of paper. Recite the oath over each paper once, then throw the paper into the fire. The incense used should be **male frankincense**, **benzoin**, and **coriander**, while choosing an appropriate astrological time.

->### ( And This is the Oath )<-

In the name of God, the Gracious, the Merciful. Praise be to God, Lord of the Worlds, the praise of those who give thanks—a praise that brings me favor with the Lord of the Worlds. He who revives the bones when they are decayed, and named Himself the Gracious, the Merciful. His name is a healing for every ailment. Master of the Day of Judgment! You alone we worship by confession, and You alone we ask for help; in You we trust. We bear witness that there is no god but You, and we bear witness that our master Muhammad is Your servant and messenger. Guide us to the straight path—the path of those whom You have favored, of the prophets, the martyrs, and the righteous; not of those who have earned Your anger, nor of those who go astray. Amen.

The mountains were crushed by **K.H.Y.'A.S.** <note>original: "كهيعص" – These are the "Mysterious Letters" (Muqatta'at) that open Chapter 19 of the Quran</note>, the Gracious, the Merciful! Where is the escape from the awe of God? Where is the escape from death? Where is the escape on the Day when He addresses Himself after the passing of His creation? Where is the escape on the Day **Israfel** blows the Trumpet? Where is the escape on "the Day a man shall flee from his brother, and his mother and his father, and his wife and his children; for every man among them that Day will be a matter to preoccupy him"? <note>Quoting Quran 80:34-37</note>. Speed, speed, before time runs out and the Lord’s wrath falls upon you! Approach and do what I have commanded you, or "there will be sent against you a flame of fire and smoke, and you will not be able to defend yourselves," and a thunderbolt like the thunderbolt of **'Ad and Thamud**, as God informed in the Quran: "as if he had fallen from the sky and the birds snatched him, or the wind blew him to a remote place." Speed! This very hour!

<meta>This page continues the sequence of magical evocations from the previous page, transitioning from the "Great Milanosy Oath" to specific "Zajr" (rebuking or compelling) incantations and a ritual for spirit mediumship (Sar'). ## ( And This is the Rebuking )

I rebuke you by the name of God, the Great, the Most Grand, whose beginning is **God** <note>original: "آل" (Al) – an ancient Semitic term for 'God' or 'Deity'.</note> and whose end is **God**, and by the name by which He created you and which is written upon your necks: it is the right of **I am that I am, the Lord, the Lord of Hosts, God Almighty** <note>original: "أهيا شراهيا أدوناي أصباؤت آل شداى" – these are the Arabic transliterations of the Hebrew divine names Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh, Adonai, Tzabaoth, and El Shaddai.</note>.

## ( Chapter on a Proven Method of Spirit-Summoning )

Write the following upon the palm of the hand:
On the thumb of the right hand: *Atal*, answer O **Mudhahhab** <note>The spiritual king of Sunday, associated with the Sun.</note>.
On the index finger of the right hand: *Batal*, answer O **Murrah** <note>The spiritual king of Monday, associated with the Moon.</note>.
On the middle finger of the right hand: *Atul*, answer O **Ahmar** <note>The spiritual king of Tuesday, associated with Mars.</note>.
On the ring finger of the right hand: *Tul*, answer O **Burqan** <note>The spiritual king of Wednesday, associated with Mercury.</note>.
On the pinky finger of the right hand: *Shayrash*, answer O **Shamhurish** <note>The spiritual king of Thursday, associated with Jupiter.</note>.

Then write upon the "kidney" of the hand <note>The fleshy part of the palm below the pinky.</note>: Answer O **Abyad** <note>The spiritual king of Friday, associated with Venus.</note>. And upon the "cavity" of the one being summoned: Answer O **Maymun** <note>The spiritual king of Saturday, associated with Saturn.</note>.

Write in the center of the palm: "Nun. By the pen and what they write" <note>Quran 68:1</note>. Around it, write: *Aflakh* (twice), *Quddus* (The Holy One) (twice). "The celestial spheres trembled from the fear of His assault," and "With truth We have sent it down, and with truth it has descended" <note>Quran 17:105</note>. Answer, O **Qarin** <note>A 'companion' spirit or double believed to reside with every human.</note>, clothe the palm and separate the fingers. Then recite the incantation 21 times. The incense to be used is **male frankincense** <term>Luban dhakar</term> and **coriander** <term>Kibra</term>. This is the incantation you must recite:

> *Atal, Batal, Atul, Tuli, Shayrash*. **Hanan** and **Mannan** <note>Divine attributes: The Affectionate and the Bestower.</note>, answer by the right of the **Chapter of the All-Merciful** <note>Surah Ar-Rahman</note>, and by the right of the prayer I have prayed over the shirt, that you might clothe the body and cast it to the ground without harm or agitation, by the right of the Might of God and by what the Pen has written from God unto the best of God's creation, Muhammad son of Abdullah. When the person falls into a trance, make them speak by saying:
> 
> Speak, by the right of Him who made the ant speak to Solomon son of David, peace be upon them both, and by the right of Him who made Jesus speak in the cradle as a child.

To dismiss the spirit: Recite the **Chapter of the Earthquake** <note>Surah Al-Zalzalah</note> 7 times and command the spirit to depart.

## ( Chapter on the Service of King Ma’ruf )

He is the judge over the seven earthly kings. If you wish to work with him, purify your clothes and your body. Recite the oath after every prayer 21 times while practicing <term>Riyada</term> <note>A period of spiritual discipline involving fasting from animal products and seclusion.</note>, beginning on a Monday. Recite the **Chapter of Sincerity** <note>Surah Al-Ikhlas, also known as Al-Samadiyya.</note> before the names. Continue this until Thursday night. After the evening prayer, enter your **seclusion** <term>Khalwa</term> and say:

"I intend to perform spiritual retreat." When you sit, say: "In the name of God, and praise be to God." Face the East and pray six units of prayer (rak'ahs).
The first unit: Recite the Opening Chapter and "Indeed, We have granted you a clear triumph, that God may forgive you your sins of the past and the future, and complete His favor upon you, and guide you on a straight path, and that God may aid you with a mighty victory" <note>Quran 48:1-3</note>.

The second unit: Recite the Opening Chapter and the **Chapter of Sincerity** 11 times, then perform the salutation.
The third unit: Recite the Opening Chapter and "until, when they reach it..." until "...dwelling therein eternally" <note>Quran 39:73</note>.
The fourth unit: Recite the Opening Chapter and the **Chapter of Sincerity** 9 times, then perform the salutation.
The fifth unit: Recite the Opening Chapter and "They love them as they love God..." until "...the Almighty, the All-Wise" <note>Quran 2:165</note>.
The sixth unit: Recite the Opening Chapter and the **Chapter of Sincerity** 7 times, then perform the salutation. (Then say):

"Swiftly" 91 times. "Exaltedly" 71 times. "Obediently" 51 times. Then say: "Answer, O **Ma'ruf**, swiftly and obediently" 51 times.

### (A Proven Benefit for Removing Fleas)

Take a Persian reed and smear it with the milk of a she-donkey and the fat of a male goat. Plant it in the middle of the house, then recite 25 times the following: "I adjure you, O fleas, for you are a troop among the troops of God from the time of 'Ad and Thamud. I adjure you by the Creator of Existence, the Unique, the Eternal, the Worshipped, that you gather upon this wood. I give you covenants and treaties that I shall not kill parent or newborn among you." They will indeed gather. When they have swarmed the wood, take it and throw it in another place; do not kill any of them, or the secret <term>sirr</term> <note>The "Secret" refers to the occult potency that makes the charm work.</note> will be nullified. Then sweep the house and recite 40 times:

->**"And why should we not rely upon Allah while He has guided us to our ways? And we will surely be patient against whatever harm you should cause us. And upon Allah let those who would rely [indeed] rely."** <note>Quoting Quran 14:12.</note><-

If this is done, no flea shall ever enter this house again. This is a subtle secret. It is completed.

### (Another Method for Fleas)

Take a cup of water and recite over it seven times the words of the Exalted: *And why should we not rely upon Allah...* (the verse). Then say: "If you are believers, then withhold your evil and your harm from us." Then sprinkle it around your bed, and you shall sleep safely from their evil.

**Hussein ibn Ishaq** <note>Referring to Hunayn ibn Ishaq (809–873 AD), a famous physician and translator of Greek works into Arabic.</note> said: A trick for driving away fleas is to take some sulfur and rhubarb and fumigate the house with them; they will flee. Or, dig a hole in the house and place **oleander leaves** <note>original: "waraq al-dafla"</note> in it, and they will all take refuge there. **Al-Razi** <note>Rhazes (865–925 AD), one of the most famous Persian physicians and polymaths of the Islamic Golden Age.</note> said: Sprinkle the house with a decoction of **black cumin** <note>original: "al-shoniz" – Nigella sativa.</note>, for it kills its fleas.

### (A Benefit for Relieving the Throb)

Extend your hand over the head of the seeker. Hold them with the thumb and forefinger and say:

In the name of God, the best of names. In the name of God, blessing and brilliance. In the name of God who raised the heavens. In the name of God who spread the earth by His power over the water. In the name of God, with whose name nothing in the earth or the heavens can cause harm. In the name of God who taught Adam the names. *In the name of God is its course and its anchorage. Indeed, my Lord is Forgiving and Merciful.* <note>Quoting Quran 11:41, the prayer of Noah.</note> *And if He willed, He could make it still.* *And to Him belongs whatever dwells in the night and the day, and He is the Hearing, the Knowing.* Be still and depart, O **Throb** <note>original: "Al-Darib" – refers to throbbing pain, such as a migraine or a pulsating abscess.</note>, by a thousand-thousand "There is no power or strength except in God, the High, the Great." This is read 7 times.

### (A Benefit for Swelling the Oppressor)

(Write the following on a small leather pouch <note>Original: *jarab*, a traditional pouch often made of hide.</note>. Write the name of the person you desire and his mother’s name on the neck of the pouch, and write the following on the rest of the pouch:

*Tahubin, Tubin, Kamashin, Almashin, Taytamushin.* <note>These are magical names of power common in the "science of letters" (ilm al-huruf), likely of Syriac or Hebrew origin.</note> **"Cursed is man; how ungrateful is he! From what substance did He create him? From a sperm-drop He created him and destined for him; Then He eased the way for him; Then He caused his death and provides a grave for him."** <note>Quran, Surah Abasa 80:17–21. In this context, the verses are used to remind the oppressor of their humble origins and mortality.</note> Then you blow into the pouch, and the target will swell up. You must charge it with this incantation, saying:

> I adjure you, O nine spoilers who cause corruption in the land <note>A reference to Surah An-Naml 27:48, referring to those who plotted against the prophet Salih.</note>, answer! O *Hawshin, Kamushin*, by the Mighty *Tahtalyun, Ana Barhana, Inayinil* (2 times), *Tub* (2 times), *Abwab* (2 times), *Bazhaf* (2 times), by the right of the King *Bashmakh Shammakh*.

The incense to be used is **sandarach** <note>A resin from the Tetraclinis tree, similar to pine resin.</note> and **male frankincense**.

---

### (A Benefit for Swelling the Thief)

Write upon the middle of a pouch the **Throne Verse** <note>Ayat al-Kursi (Quran 2:255), considered the most powerful protective verse in the Quran.</note> in <term>disconnected letters</term> <note>Writing words as individual letters (e.g., A-L-L-A-H) is a common technique in Arabic magic to release the spiritual energy of the words.</note> along with the names of the seven prophets, who are: (**Noah, Lot, Salih, Abraham, Moses, Jesus, Muhammad, peace be upon him**). Then recite the Throne Verse. Then say: "O Allah, I ask You by the right of this Prophet (and point with your finger to the name of **Noah**) and by that which You sent with this Prophet, that You swell the belly of the thief of the belongings of [Name]." Then blow into the pouch slightly. Then recite the Throne Verse again and do as you did before, pointing your finger to the second prophet, and so on until the seven prophets are completed, blowing a little for each prophet. Then tie the pouch and fix it against the wall. The thief who is the subject of this work will find his belly swelling; this is a proven method.

---

### (A Proven Benefit for Subjecting Whomever You Desire)

This is a sublime benefit regarding His Name, the Exalted: **"The Loving"** (*Al-Wadud*), for <term>subjection</term> <note>Taskheer: the magical act of making someone or something compliant or devoted to one's will.</note> of whomever you wish and for attracting their heart. If you desire this, fast for the sake of Allah the Exalted on Thursday, under the condition of <term>spiritual discipline</term> <note>Riyada: a period of fasting and retreat, usually avoiding animal products and focusing on prayer.</note>. When it is Friday night, after the evening prayer and before you pray the Witr <note>The final prayer of the night.</note>, perform two units of prayer. In the first unit, after the Opening (*Al-Fatiha*), recite **Surah Al-Buruj** <note>Chapter 85, "The Constellations."</note>. In the second unit, after the Opening, recite **Surah Ash-Sharh** <note>Chapter 94, "The Solace."</note>. You must say **"O Loving One"** (*Ya Wadud*) during the standing, sitting, bowing, and prostrating until the two units are finished. 

After that, take 20 **chickpeas**. Recite the Name (**O Loving One**) 1,000 times over each chickpea while holding it in your hand, and blow upon it. Then, at the head of every thousand, recite this supplication:

> O Allah, I ask You by Your name, The Loving, to subject to me the heart of [Name] son/daughter of [Mother's Name] with love, affection, compassion, obedience, acceptance, subjection, guidance, and a permanent, intense, burning love. Place his/her forelock in my hand and according to my will; surely You are capable of all things.

Then tie them in a rag and recite **Surah Al-Ikhlas** <note>Chapter 112, "Sincerity."</note> 40 times and **Surah Ya-Sin** <note>Chapter 36, often called the "Heart of the Quran."</note> 7 times over them. Then bury them near a constant fire until the desired result is achieved. Keep this secret, for it is proven.

## A Benefit from the Secrets of the Triple Square

This is what is read before every operation, and you must adopt it as a **litany** <note>original: *wird*. A regular, rhythmic practice of devotion or prayer.</note>.

If it is for the daily litany, you should pray two <term>rak'ahs</term> <note>Units of Islamic prayer consisting of standing, bowing, and prostrating.</note> for the sake of God Almighty. In the first unit, read the **Opening** <note>Surah Al-Fatihah, the first chapter of the Quran.</note> and **"O You Disbelievers"** <note>Surah Al-Kafirun (109).</note>. In the second unit, read the **Opening** and **"Sincerity"** <note>Surah Al-Ikhlas (112).</note>. This should be done after the Dawn prayer or after the Sunset prayer. Then, read the following **Prayer for Forgiveness** (Istighfar):

> Glory be to God and by His praise; Glory be to God the Almighty. I seek forgiveness from God for that which my Lord does not love and which does not please Him, for myself, my parents, and for those who have rights over me, in every breath and every blink of an eye, as many times as is encompassed by the knowledge of God. **(Repeat 70 times)**.

Then, offer blessings upon the Prophet as follows:

> O Allah, bless, grant peace, and bestow baraka <note>Divine blessing or grace.</note> upon our master Muhammad—the prayer of a confused and needy servant who has cried out from distress and hardship and taken refuge at the gate of the Generous One, so that the gates of relief were opened for him—and upon his family and companions, and grant them peace.

Then you pray the two previously mentioned units of prayer before the prayer for forgiveness, and then begin reading these noble verses, which are:

->In the name of God, the Compassionate, the Merciful<-

> O Allah, I ask You for guidance and success. There is no creature but that He holds its forelock. Indeed, my Lord is on a straight path." <note>Quran, Surah Hud 11:56.</note> "And with Him are the keys of the unseen; none knows them except Him. And He knows what is on the land and in the sea. Not a leaf falls but that He knows it. And no grain is there within the darknesses of the earth and no moist or dry [thing] but that it is written in a clear record." <note>Quran, Surah Al-An'am 6:59.</note> "And, [O Muhammad], you are not [engaged] in any matter or recite any of the Qur'an and you [people] do not do any deed except that We are witness over you when you are involved in it. And not absent from your Lord is any atom's weight within the earth or within the heaven or [anything] smaller than that or greater but that it is in a clear record." <note>Quran, Surah Yunus 10:61.</note> "Say, 'None in the heavens and earth knows the unseen except God,'" <note>Quran, Surah Al-Naml 27:65.</note> and "God will bring forth that which you were concealing." <note>Quran, Surah Al-Baqarah 2:72.</note> God the Almighty has spoken the truth.

Then you read this poetic verse **693 times**, which is:

->O Allah, O our Lord, O Possessor of the **hidden kindness**<-
->By the right of the **Subtle One**, we seek help and we find sufficiency<-

<meta>This page transitions from the devotional prayers and general talismans for love on page 98 into the technical "Science of Letters" (Ilm al-Huruf). It introduces the Ayqagh system, a method of categorizing the Arabic alphabet for ritual and talismanic use.</meta>

# Chapter Regarding the Mothers of Letters

Know that the "Mothers of Letters" encompass the twenty-eight letters, including their ranks, days, governing spirits, and the Beautiful Names of God associated with them. There are nine ranks corresponding to the nine groups of letters. Each rank has a specific day, a moving planet, two noble Names from the Beautiful Names of God, a suitable day for ritual work, and a geometric shape to be observed. <note>The "Mothers of Letters" refers to a system where the alphabet is divided into groups based on their numerical values in the Abjad system.</note>

-> (These are the ranks and days as you see) <-

| A-Y-Q-Gh | B-K-R | J-L-Sh | D-M-T | H-N-Th | W-S-Kh | Z-'A-Dh | H-F-D | T-S-Z |
| :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
| 1111 | 222 | 333 | 444 | 555 | 666 | 777 | 888 | 999 |

<note>The letters in the table follow the "Ayqagh" sequence, which groups letters by their decimal place values: for example, Alif (1), Ya (10), Qaf (100), and Ghayn (1000) total 1111.</note>

When you desire to perform a specific ritual, take the rank of these letters and extract their numbers in their total, separated, and expanded forms. Add to them the total number of the letters and the numerical value of the two noble Names. Once the calculation is complete, choose a time that corresponds to the day intended for the work. Write it upon gazelle parchment using musk, saffron, and rosewater. Your work should take place during the waxing moon, from the first of the lunar month until the eighteenth. Then, write the letters individually in a circle around the seal. This requires a spiritual retreat of seven days, during which you employ the spiritual and celestial angels. I shall make the subject clearer for you in the following explanation, and I say, and with God is all success:

Know that the letters **A-Y-Q-Gh** <note>original: أيقغ</note> are associated with Sunday, the planet Sun, the hexagon <term>al-musaddas</term> <note>A six-by-six magic square.</note>, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Ever-Living, The Self-Subsisting** <note>al-Hayy, al-Qayyum</note>. Its apparent number is 1111.

**B-K-R** <note>original: بكر</note> has the number 222, is associated with Monday, the planet Moon, the triangle <term>al-muthallath</term> <note>A three-by-three magic square.</note>, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Compassionate, The Merciful** <note>al-Rahman, al-Rahim</note>.

**J-L-Sh** <note>original: جلش</note> has the number 333, is associated with Tuesday, the planet Mars, the nonagon <term>al-mutassa'</term> <note>A nine-by-nine magic square.</note>, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Sovereign, The Most Holy** <note>al-Malik, al-Quddus</note>.

**D-M-T** <note>original: دمت</note> has the number 444, is associated with Wednesday, the planet Mercury, the square <term>al-murabba'</term> <note>A four-by-four magic square.</note>, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Great, The Most Exalted** <note>al-Kabir, al-Muta'al</note>.

**H-N-Th** <note>original: هنث</note> has the number 555, is associated with Thursday, the hexagon <term>al-musaddas</term>, the planet Jupiter, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Opener, The Provider** <note>al-Fattah, al-Razzaq</note>.

**W-S-Kh** <note>original: وسخ</note> has the number 666, is associated with Friday, the planet Venus, the pentagon <term>al-mukhammas</term> <note>A five-by-five magic square.</note>, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Severe, The Possessor of Strength** <note>al-Shadid, Dhu al-Quwwa</note>.

**Z-'A-Dh** <note>original: زعذ</note> has the number 777, is associated with Saturday, the planet Saturn, the heptagon <term>al-musabba'</term> <note>A seven-by-seven magic square.</note>, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Strong, The Capable** <note>al-Qawi, al-Qadir</note>.

**H-F-D** <note>original: حفض</note> has the number 888, is associated with Sunday, the planet Sun, the hexagon <term>al-musaddas</term>, and the Beautiful Names of God: **The Strong, The Subduer** <note>al-Qawi, al-Qahhar</note>.

<meta>This page continues the technical instructions for the "Science of Letters" (Ilm al-Huruf) introduced on the previous page. While page 99 defined the nine ranks of the alphabet, this page focuses on the practical "conditions" for the ritual and the complex mathematical expansion of the first rank (Ayfagh) and the Divine Names associated with it.</meta>

### (Its Conditions)

You must be pure in garment, body, and place, maintaining the prayers and remaining in a state of humble seclusion and expectation. The system of spiritual discipline <note>original: "riyada" — a period of ascetic practice or retreat</note> for each rank lasts seven days, during which you must be fasting. Place the magic square <note>original: "wafq"</note> every night under the stars and perfume it with the incense of its specific planet every morning and evening. Recite the two Divine Names according to the numerical value of the magic square while burning incense during the day after the dawn prayer, after noon, at the time of the afternoon prayer, and in the heart of the night. Repeat this work according to this system for seven days.

Know that spiritual discipline, patience, fasting, and prayer are among the essential conditions, as is the keeping of secrets. I shall now explain to you the ranks, the Names, their movements, their totals, their expansions, and their detailed forms with brevity and conciseness. I say, and with God lies all success:

### The First Rank (Ayfagh) — its expansion is as follows:

| A | L | F | Y | A | Q | A | F | Gh | Y | N |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 30 | 80 | 10 | 1 | 100 | 1 | 80 | 1000 | 10 | 50 |

<note>This table shows the "expansion" (basat) of the letters Alif (1), Ya (10), Qaf (100), and Ghayn (1000). Each letter's name is spelled out and then assigned a value based on the Abjad system.</note>

One, thirty, eighty, ten, one, one hundred, one, eighty, one thousand, ten, fifty — sum all of these according to the calculation of the camel <note>term: "hisab al-jumal" — the Abjad system where each Arabic letter has a numerical value</note> and add to them the number of the letters, which is 1111, and take the sum as a single total.

### Then extract the two Names (The Living, The Self-Sustaining)
<note>original: "Hayy Qayyum"</note>

Their total value is 174. Their detailed spelling is (Ha, Ya, Qaf, Ya, Waw, Mim). **(Its expansion)** is: eight, ten, one hundred, ten, six, forty. <note>These numbers represent the numerical values of the letters that spell out the Divine Names "Hayy" and "Qayyum".</note> The number of letters is 16. Thus, the total value of the two noble Names is 174, the value of the detailed spelling is 315, and their expansion is 4699. The grand total for all these names combined is 5188. The final result emerging from this rank and the Names is the number 18166. Its magic square is a hexagon <note>term: "wafq musaddas" — a 6x6 grid talisman</note>; its day is Sunday. This explanation is sufficient, and God knows best.

### (Section on the "Ahwaj Zubda" Magic Square)

I have seen this great magic square in many books, but nothing was mentioned except its layout. It is impossible for a student to benefit from it that way, so I desired to clarify it for you with all its conditions and the method of working with it.

Know—may God Almighty guide you to His obedience—that if you desire harmony and love between two people who hate each other, there must be a "natural composition" based on letter correspondences. Through this, harmony and inclination occur by the permission of God Almighty, provided you fulfill the conditions according to the established laws agreed upon by the scholars of this art. Here is an example so you may judge its layout; do not focus merely on its nature, for the connection of the layout to the nature is like the connection of the condition to the thing conditioned.

->( Example of that )<-

<insert>Ahwaj Zabda</insert> <note>original: "أحوج زبده" — a mnemonic phrase used to remember a specific sequence of letters and their numerical values in the Abjad system.</note>

If we desire that Ahmad loves Muhammad, the arrangement would be thus:

Ahmad, son of Fatima, loves Muhammad, son of Aisha; [by] God, Gabriel, the Guide, the Loving.

One must observe the Quranic meanings, the Most Beautiful Names of God, and the verses appropriate for the purpose, such as His saying (the Exalted): "And I cast love upon you from Me" <note>Quran 20:39</note>; and His saying: "They love them and He loves them" <note>Quran 5:54</note>, and similar Quranic verses.

Then take the numerical values of all those letters and form the "natural table" using the sequence of *Ahwaj Zabda*, which consists of the individual letters of the *Abjad Hawwaz* sequence up to the letter *Ha* (ح). These letters are a mixture of odd and even [numbers] and perfect opposites. Then sum them according to the *Abjad* calculation <term>Abjad calculation: A system where each letter of the Arabic alphabet is assigned a specific numerical value, allowing words to be converted into numbers.</term> and divide the total exactly in half. Then subtract only the number 8 from one of the halves—which represents the letters of the *Ahwaj Zabda* sequence—and what remains, you place in the "House of Ta" (ط) in the table. This is the ninth house in the numerical order, which is the second cell of the third horizontal row. Continue from there by adding one—which is the "Base" <note>original: "al-as"</note>—until the table is complete. 

Then examine every row, both horizontally and vertically. The total sum of the calculation is called the "Cube" <term>Cube: In the context of this art, the constant sum of any row, column, or diagonal in the magic square.</term> in the terminology of this art. Likewise, check its right and left diagonals. If the sums do not match, then an error has entered your work, so re-examine it. However, if the total number is odd, such as 75 for example, subtract one from it (the Base) and take half of the remainder, which is 37. Place this in the "House of Ta" as previously mentioned. When you reach the thirteenth house, add the one you previously subtracted from the original total to that cell and complete the work. Then test it; if the totals are consistent, your work is finished.

->( Continuation of the mentioned example )<-

Ahmad, son of Fatima, loves Muhammad, son of Aisha; [by] God, Gabriel, the Guide, the Loving. Its expansion by the Great Calculation <note>original: "al-jumal al-kabir"</note> is 1142. Half of that number is 571. If we subtract 8 from it, 563 remains. Thus the total sum is 1142 and the "Key" <note>The starting number for the square</note> is 563. This is the example of the "natural square" <note>original: "al-wafq al-tabi'i"</note>; know this and master it to succeed with this secret.

Once you have completed the arrangement, return to the original total, which is 1142, and divide it by 7. If 1 remains, the work must be done on Sunday; if 2 remains, Monday; if 3 remains, Tuesday; if 4 remains, Wednesday; if 5 remains, Thursday; if 6 remains, Friday; and if 7 remains, Saturday. Once you know the day on which to draw it, subtract 12 from the original total repeatedly <note>original: "12-12"</note> until you have a remainder less than 12; this remainder is the hour. Then divide that remainder by 3. If 1 remains, perfume the square with an incense of animal origin; if 2 remains, use a plant-based incense; and if 3 remains, use a mineral-based incense.

Then you return to the nature of the letters and weigh them with the "Philosophical Scale" to identify the dominant nature among the Four Natures <term>Four Natures: The classical elements of Fire, Air, Water, and Earth, which were believed to govern the spiritual and physical properties of all things.</term>.

If the dominant element is **Fire**, place the talisman on that same day inside a clay shard and put it near a fire beneath the ashes, so that the heat of the fire reaches it, but do not place it directly in the center of the fire. If the dominant element is **Air**, write it on a piece of paper and hang it where the wind blows. If the dominant element is **Water**, place it inside a Persian reed tube after writing it on paper, seal it with wax, and bury it in a mosque or any empty place far from people and where feet do not tread. <note>In many occult traditions, the "Water" element is usually associated with bodies of water, but here the instruction suggests burial in a quiet or sacred place.</note>

The ink [should be made of] musk, saffron, and rose water. Then, after placing it [according to its element] on its specific day and hour while incense is rising, you should recite the Most Beautiful Names of God that correspond to the aforementioned total, as well as the Noble Verses, according to the number of the "rib" (side) of the table, which is the base 1142. This is after you have derived from this number the name of the Angel appointed over this number and the table.

**(How to do this):** The original total is 1143. We subtract the number 51 from it, and the remainder is the number 1091. <note>Original arithmetic error: 1143 - 51 is actually 1092, but the text proceeds with 1091.</note> We place the thousands before the hundreds, the hundreds before the tens, and the tens before the ones. If the letters exceed one, we add a letter to the letters instead of its number for the remainder, and place it before the thousands. You pronounce the whole set as letters and add "-yail" to them. Thus, its pronunciation for 1091 is *Ghassayail* <note>Gh (1000) + Sa (90) + A (1) + yail = Ghassayail. The suffix "-yail" is the Arabic equivalent of the Hebrew "-iel," signifying a divine or angelic entity.</note>. You then charge him to take charge of fulfilling the purpose, and you adjure by the mentioned Names and Verses.

### ->( And this is the manner of his charging, you say )<-

I conjure you, O **Ghassayail**, who is appointed over these luminous letters, spiritual names, and natural composed words, by the sanctity of His Name, the Exalted, the Loving (*al-Wadud*), the Guide (*al-Hadi*), and by their right over you, that you agitate, disturb, unsettle, and move the spirituality of [Person] son of [Mother]. Haste, haste! Speed, speed! This hour, this hour! By the right of Gabriel, Michael, Israfil, and Azrael. O Guide, O Loving! Harmonize and incline the heart of [Person] son of [Mother] toward [Person] son of [Mother], by the sanctity of your Prophet Muhammad <gloss>peace and blessings be upon him</gloss> and by the right of Your Most Beautiful Names and Your Greatest Verses. (If you add to that His saying, the Exalted): *“If you had spent all that is in the earth, you could not have brought their hearts together; but Allah brought them together”* <note>Quran 8:63</note>, it would be more complete and excellent.

### ->( A benefit regarding Surah Yasin )<-

This is for every important matter and the fulfillment of any need, such as subjugation, seeking divine guidance (*Istikhara*), and other things.

Dismiss the "Resident of the Place" <term>Resident of the Place ('Amir al-Makan): In folk belief, these are spirits (jinn) that inhabit buildings; practitioners "dismiss" them rituals to ensure they do not interfere.</term>, then wait a little and begin reciting the noble Surah until you reach the words of the Exalted: *"Peace!—a word from a Merciful Lord"* <note>Quran 36:58</note>. Repeat this verse 14 times, conclude the Surah, and go to sleep. For indeed, God—Glory be to Him and the Exalted—will fulfill your need, and it is a proven method.

## (Section on Sending the Spiritual Messenger of the Basmala—Tested and Authentic)

If you desire this, write it <note>The Basmala: the phrase "In the name of God, the Gracious, the Merciful."</note> on a sheet of paper with saffron and rose water, writing the letters individually <term>Individual letters: A common practice in Islamic magic where words are broken into isolated characters (*huruof muqatta'a*) to release their spiritual power.</term> 19 times. Then, place it inside a reed, tie it around its middle with a silk thread, and hang it from the end of a bow <note>The "siya" is the curved tip of a traditional bow.</note> while burning incense. The incense is benzoin <note>original: "jaawi"</note>. Recite the Basmala 786 times. The sign of a successful response is the shaking and rotation of the reed. You should have already commissioned the servant of the Noble Basmala on the paper you wrote, stating after the Basmala: "Commission yourself, O servant of the Noble Basmala, with terror and intense dread, to [Name] son of [Mother’s Name]. Appear to him in my form and with my name so that he comes to me humbled, lowly, and submissive, fulfilling my need. Hurry, hurry! Hasten, hasten! At once, at once!"

As for the recitation, you should recite this commissioning prayer once after every hundred repetitions of the Basmala. It is this prayer:

> In the name of God, the Gracious, the Merciful. O God, I ask You by the secret of Your Greatest, All-Encompassing Name, and by Your attribute of Grace in the manifestations of Your brilliant secret, and by Your attribute of Mercy in the vast unseen of Your secret, that You support my inner being with the secret of Your Greatest Name, and my outer being with the light of Your most noble countenance. Protect me from what I fear and dread, and place me among the best of all Your servants. May God’s peace and blessings be upon our master Muhammad, first and last, outwardly and inwardly, and upon his family and companions.

## (Benefit of the Chapter of the Pleading Woman)

Read the Chapter of the Pleading Woman <note>Surah al-Mujadilah, the 58th chapter of the Quran.</note>, and every time you pass the Name of Majesty <note>The word "Allah".</note>, write it down and write beneath it the letters **Waw (و)** and **Sad (ص)**. Carry this on your person, for it brings great acceptance. If you wish to attract someone, place the paper between your palms, fold them over it, and read the Chapter of Sincerity <note>Surah al-Ikhlas, the 112th chapter of the Quran.</note> 3 times. Then touch whoever you wish, and they will follow you.

## (Section on Stopping a Millstone)

Recite the following over 7 grains of wheat and throw them into the throat of the millstone, and it will stop. This is what you say:

**Ah (٣) Yah (٣) Lah (٣) Ah (٣) Allah (٣)** <note>These are "voces magicae" or spiritual names intended for power rather than literal meaning.</note> Be still, O stone! Do not turn or move until God permits, by the right of these names.
To release it <note>original: "wahalluhu"</note>, recite over 7 grains of wheat and throw them in, saying: **Yalaf-ha (٣) Yah Ah (٣)**. Turn, O stone, by the permission of God the Exalted.

## (Section on Releasing the Bound)

Write the following on a chicken egg and also write it on a piece of paper. The bound person <term>The Bound: A person (usually a groom) believed to be rendered impotent through magic.</term> should eat the egg and carry the paper, and he will be released. This is what you write:

> In the name of God, the Gracious, the Merciful. *Unquestionably, the allies of God [unto] will not grieve.* <note>Quran 10:62</note> *Unquestionably, the promise of God is truth [unto] they do not know.* <note>Quran 10:55</note> *Beautified for mankind [unto] and sons.* <note>Quran 3:14</note> *And We left some of them that day [unto] gathered.* <note>Quran 18:99</note> *Moses said: What you have brought is magic [unto] the corrupters.* <note>Quran 10:81</note> *Falsehood cannot approach it [unto] Praiseworthy.* <note>Quran 41:42</note> Be released, O bound one, by the right of these verses.

### ( Section on Provision )

Perform a spiritual discipline <term>Riyada: A period of fasting and retreat used by practitioners to attune themselves to spiritual forces.</term> for one week. Begin your recitation on Thursday night <note>original: "Laylat al-Jumu'ah"—in the Islamic calendar, the night preceding Friday is considered the start of the holy day.</note>. On the seventh night, the servant of this noble Chapter will come to you and give you your portion.

### ( A Benefit for Reforming a Woman Inclined Toward Misconduct )

### ( A Benefit for Headaches )

Write upon a piece of onion skin <note>original: "waraqat basal" - can refer to the thin outer skin of an onion or a specific type of paper.</note> and have the person carry it; the headache and migraine will vanish. This is what you write: **Q T** <note>original: "ق ط" — these are isolated "mystery letters" used as a talismanic code.</note>.

## The Invocation of Dahrushiyah
->In the name of God, the Gracious, the Merciful<-

May God bless our master Muhammad and his family and companions and grant them peace. To proceed: This is the Invocation of Dahrushiyah <term>Dahrushiyah: A widely known conjuration in the "Science of Letters" used to command spiritual entities.</term> and its various applications. I speak, and with God is all success.

### ( The First Application )

If a man or a woman is struck down <note>Referring to a state of possession or an epileptic seizure believed to be caused by spirits.</note>, write between their eyes: **"Is then one who is a believer like one who is a transgressor? They are not equal"** <note>Quran 32:18</note> until the words **"as a welcome, as a welcome, as a welcome."** <note>Quran 32:19</note>

In their right hand, write the Seal of Solomon. That which has been proven authentic to us regarding his seal is what is drawn for you here. It has seven horns, and inside each horn is one of the letters from the set **F, J, Sh, Th, Z, Kh, Z** <note>original: "ف ج ش ث ظ خ ز" — these are the "Seven Fallen Letters" not found in the Fatiha, each associated with a planet and an angel.</note> along with these symbols:

<note>A series of magical symbols and talismans written in a single line, including three vertical lines, the letter M, three more vertical lines, a symbol resembling a large 'H', a five-pointed star, three vertical lines, the letter Ha, and a symbol resembling the number 6.</note>

Inside the seal, write: **"Is he who was dead and We gave him life..."** <note>Quran 6:122</note> to the word **"...people."** This seal holds great status among the Jinn <note>Spiritual beings made of smokeless fire.</note>. This is it, so understand and you shall be guided.

Hang this upon the patient, for it is beneficial, God willing.

->( The Second Procedure )<-

Know, may God Almighty grant you success, that those responsible for "Sar‘" <note>Historically interpreted as "epilepsy" or "falling sickness," but here specifically attributed to "shayatīn" (demons) and "ifrit" (powerful spirits).</note> are demons and spirits who wish to prevent a woman from her husband and from being alone with him; they are among the harshest and most tyrannical of spirits. Sometimes they strike the woman at the end of the month, and sometimes at the beginning. Most of them afflict women who are unable to conceive. They are of seven types:

The first type of these spirits only strikes a woman if she beautifies herself, wears perfume, or washes her body or clothes. These spirits belong to the circle of **Maymun the Black**, the servants of **The Red**, and the soldiers of **The White** <note>These are names of prominent Jinn leaders associated with specific colors and planets.</note>. If the afflicted person is made to speak, the spirit will say to you: "I am Maymun," "I am the Red," or "I am the White." 

The treatment for this is through conjurations such as the **Dahrushiyah** <term>Al-Dahrushiyah: One of the most famous and powerful "standard" invocations in medieval Arabic magic, used to compel spirits to manifest or depart.</term> which we are currently discussing. During the ritual, the woman should be behind a screen and should wear her clothes in the manner of men. She must not wear a belt or any protective amulet, nor should there be any iron in her vicinity. 

Prepare yourself, O seeker, as you stand outside the screen where the woman is, and begin the invocation while the incense rises. Write the Seal of Solomon on the hand of the afflicted woman, and on her forehead write the **Verse of Unveiling** <note>"We have stripped from you your veil, so your sight this day is sharp" (Quran 50:22).</note>. Hold the forefinger of her left hand and continue reciting the Dahrushiyah until the spirit speaks. When it speaks, ask it which type of spirit it is. If it is of the night-spirits, leave it until nightfall; if it is of the day-spirits, leave it until the day. 

The incense to be used is as follows: If it is **Maymun the Black**, use skull incense <note>original: "bakhur al-jamjamah"</note> or Sudanese incense. If it is **The Red**, use Red Myrrh or Java Frankincense. If it is **The White**, use Mastic, or its equivalent, or Long Pepper, or Cubeb. Finally, write the Seal of Solomon in an amulet and hang it in that place. Ensure there are no children present nor anyone who speaks excessively.

### (The Third Procedure)

Regarding the treatment of one <note>The "one" refers to a possessing spirit or *Arid*.</note> who strikes a beautiful woman in her posterior and intends to reside in that location: Perform for her the ritual of "The Veil" <note>original: "al-Satr"</note> and recite the "Dahrushiyah" <note>A specific powerful conjuration or oath used in Islamic occultism to command spirits.</note> and so on.
